<script>
function goBack() {
  if (window.history.length > 1) window.history.back();
  else window.location.href = 'chizen.html';
}

(function() {
  var c = {phone:'+967 77 123 4567', whatsapp:'967771234567', email:'support@chizen.com'};
  try {
    var s = localStorage.getItem('chizen_contact');
    if (s) c = Object.assign(c, JSON.parse(s));
  } catch(e) {}

  var el = document.getElementById('contact-info');
  if (el) {
    el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="contact-row"><div class="contact-icon">📱</div><div class="contact-text"><div class="contact-label">الهاتف</div><div class="contact-value">' + c.phone + '</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="contact-row"><div class="contact-icon">💬</div><div class="contact-text"><div class="contact-label">واتساب</div><div class="contact-value">+' + c.whatsapp + '</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="contact-row"><div class="contact-icon">✉️</div><div class="contact-text"><div class="contact-label">البريد الإلكتروني</div><div class="contact-value">' + c.email + '</div></div></div>';
  }

  var wa = document.getElementById('wa-btn');
  var tel = document.getElementById('tel-btn');
  var mail = document.getElementById('mail-btn');
  if (wa) wa.href = 'https://wa.me/' + c.whatsapp;
  if (tel) tel.href = 'tel:' + c.phone.replace(/\s/g,'');
  if (mail) mail.href = 'mailto:' + c.email;

  try {
    var products = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_fb_products') || '[]');
    var users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_users') || '[]');
    var cats = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_categories') || '[]');

    var pEl = document.getElementById('stat-products');
    var uEl = document.getElementById('stat-users');
    var cEl = document.getElementById('stat-cats');
    if (pEl) pEl.textContent = products.length > 0 ? products.length : 16;
    if (uEl) uEl.textContent = users.length > 0 ? users.length : 1;
    if (cEl) cEl.textContent = cats.length > 0 ? cats.length : 8;
  } catch(e) {}

  try {
    var st = localStorage.getItem('chizen_state');
    if (st) {
      var p = JSON.parse(st);
      if (p.theme === 'light') document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
      document.documentElement.lang = p.lang || 'ar';
      document.documentElement.dir = (p.lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr');
    }
  } catch(e) {}
})();
</script>

<div class="modal" id="modal" onclick="if(event.target===this)APP.closeModal()"><div class="ms"><div class="mh"></div><div class="mt2" id="modal-t"></div><div id="modal-c"></div></div></div>
<div class="dlg" id="dlg" onclick="if(event.target===this)APP.closeDlg()"><div class="dlb" id="dlb"></div></div>
<div class="toast" id="toast"></div>
<script src="https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-app-compat.js"></script>
<script src="https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth-compat.js"></script>
<script src="https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore-compat.js"></script>
<script src="firebase-config.js"></script>
<script src="firebase-service.js"></script>
<script>
function esc(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return{'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];});}
var APP={
  state:{lang:'ar',theme:'dark',notifications:true,offers:true,orderNotifs:true,biometric:false,fontSize:'medium',user:null},
  cart:[],orders:[],favorites:[],notifications:[],
  products:[],banners:[],cats:[],banks:[],
  coupons:[],shipping:{fee:0,freeAbove:0},appliedCoupon:null,
  pickupLocations:null,selectedPickup:null,currentPickupTab:'aden',currentDistrict:null,
  currentProd:null,currentQty:1,selectedBank:null,currentSc:'home',
  
  init:async function(){
    var self=this;
    try{var s=localStorage.getItem('chizen_state');if(s){var p=JSON.parse(s);for(var k in p)if(p[k]!==undefined&&p[k]!==null)self.state[k]=p[k];}}catch(e){}
    try{self.cart=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_cart')||'[]');}catch(e){}
    try{self.favorites=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_favorites')||'[]');}catch(e){}
    self.applyState();
    self.syncBg();
    self.setupHistory();
  },
  
  syncBg:function(){
    var self=this;
    if(!window.FB||!FB.ready){setTimeout(function(){self.syncBg();},200);return;}
    if(FB.currentUser){self.onUserReady(FB.currentUser);return;}
    FB.auth.onAuthStateChanged(function(u){
      if(u){self.onUserReady(u);}
      else{setTimeout(function(){if(!FB.currentUser)window.location.replace('index.html');},3000);}
    });
  },
  
  onUserReady:function(u){
    var self=this;
    self.state.user={name:u.displayName||'User',email:u.email||'',uid:u.uid};
    self.updateUserUI();
    if(window.FB){
      FB.getUserProfile().then(function(p){if(p&&p.loyaltyPoints)self.state.loyaltyPoints=p.loyaltyPoints;self.updateLoyalty();});
      FB.getProducts().then(function(d){if(d&&d.length){self.products=d;self.renderProducts();self.renderFlash();}});
      FB.getConfig('banners').then(function(b){if(b&&b.list){self.banners=b.list;self.renderBanners();}});
      FB.getConfig('categories').then(function(c){if(c&&c.list){self.cats=c.list;self.renderCats();}});
      FB.getConfig('payments').then(function(p){if(p&&p.list){self.banks=p.list;self.renderBanks();}});
      FB.getConfig('coupons').then(function(c){if(c&&c.list)self.coupons=c.list;});
      FB.getConfig('shipping').then(function(s){if(s)self.shipping=s;});
      FB.getUserOrders().then(function(o){if(o){self.orders=o;self.renderOrders();self.renderTrack();}});
      FB.getNotifications().then(function(n){if(n){self.notifications=n;self.updateNotifB();}});
    }
    setTimeout(function(){var sp=document.getElementById('splash');if(sp){sp.style.opacity='0';setTimeout(function(){sp.remove();},500);}},500);
  },
  
  updateUserUI:function(){
    var u=this.state.user;if(!u)return;
    var n=document.getElementById('home-user');if(n)n.textContent=u.name;
    var pn=document.getElementById('prof-n');if(pn)pn.textContent=u.name;
    var pe=document.getElementById('prof-e');if(pe)pe.textContent=u.email;
    var pa=document.getElementById('prof-av');if(pa)pa.textContent=u.name.charAt(0);
  },
  
  updateLoyalty:function(){
    var p=this.state.loyaltyPoints||0;
    var el=document.getElementById('loyalty');if(el)el.textContent=p+' نقطة';
    var pp=document.getElementById('prof-pts');if(pp)pp.textContent=p+' نقطة';
  },
  
  applyState:function(){
    document.documentElement.lang=this.state.lang;
    document.documentElement.dir=this.state.lang==='ar'?'rtl':'ltr';
    if(this.state.theme==='light')document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme','light');
    else document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
  },
  
  saveState:function(){try{localStorage.setItem('chizen_state',JSON.stringify(this.state));}catch(e){}},
  
  setupHistory:function(){
    try{history.replaceState({s:'home'},'','#home');}catch(e){}
    var self=this;
    window.addEventListener('popstate',function(){if(self.currentSc!=='home'){self.navigate('home',false);}});
  },
  
  navigate:function(id,push){
    if(push===undefined)push=true;
    if(this.currentSc===id)return;
    this.currentSc=id;
    document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(function(s){s.classList.remove('active');});
    var t=document.getElementById('sc-'+id);if(t){t.classList.add('active');t.scrollTop=0;}
    var hide=['details','checkout','success','settings','points','notifications','favorites','help'];
    var nav=document.getElementById('bnav');
    if(nav)nav.style.display=hide.indexOf(id)>-1?'none':'flex';
    var map={home:0,cart:1,orders:2,profile:3};
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(n,i){if(i===map[id])n.classList.add('active');else n.classList.remove('active');});
    if(id==='cart')this.renderCart();
    if(id==='orders')this.renderOrders();
    if(id==='favorites')this.renderFav();
    if(id==='notifications')this.renderNotifs();
    if(id==='points')this.renderPoints();
    if(id==='help')this.renderHelp();
    if(id==='settings')this.renderSettings();
  },
  
  goTab:function(id){this.navigate(id,true);},
  closeModal:function(){document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('active');},
  closeDlg:function(){document.getElementById('dlg').classList.remove('active');},
  
  renderCats:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('cats');if(!c)return;
    c.innerHTML=this.cats.map(function(cat){return '<div class="cat" onclick="APP.filterCat(\''+esc(cat.key)+'\')"><div class="cat-i">'+esc(cat.icon||'📦')+'</div><div class="cat-n">'+esc(cat.name)+'</div></div>';}).join('');
  },
  
  renderProducts:function(list){
    var c=document.getElementById('prods');if(!c)return;
    if(!list)list=this.products;
    if(!list.length){c.innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--mt);">لا توجد منتجات</div>';return;}
    c.innerHTML=list.map(function(p){
      var fav=APP.favorites.indexOf(p.id)>-1;
      return '<div class="prod" onclick="APP.openProduct(\''+esc(p.id)+'\')"><div class="prod-b">'+esc(p.badge||'')+'</div><div class="prod-f'+(fav?' active':'')+'" onclick="event.stopPropagation();APP.togFav(\''+esc(p.id)+'\',this)"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M19 14c1.49-1.46 3-3.21 3-5.5A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 16.5 3c-1.76 0-3 .5-4.5 2-1.5-1.5-2.74-2-4.5-2A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 2 8.5c0 2.3 1.5 4.05 3 5.5l7 7Z"/></svg></div><div class="prod-i"><img src="'+esc(p.img||'')+'" loading="lazy"></div><div class="prod-inf"><div class="prod-n">'+esc(p.name)+'</div><div class="pr"><span class="price">'+esc(p.price)+'</span><span class="price-c">ر.س</span>'+(p.old?'<span class="price-o">'+esc(p.old)+'</span>':'')+'</div></div></div>';
    }).join('');
  },
  
  renderFlash:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('flash-c');if(!c)return;
    var items=this.products.filter(function(p){return p.old&&(p.old-p.price)/p.old>0.15;}).slice(0,6);
    c.innerHTML=items.map(function(p){return '<div class="prod" onclick="APP.openProduct(\''+esc(p.id)+'\')"><div class="prod-i"><img src="'+esc(p.img||'')+'"><div class="prod-b">⚡ '+esc(p.badge)+'</div></div><div class="prod-inf"><div class="prod-n">'+esc(p.name)+'</div><div class="pr"><span class="price">'+esc(p.price)+'</span><span class="price-o">'+esc(p.old)+'</span></div></div></div>';}).join('');
  },
  
  renderBanners:function(){
    var s=document.getElementById('slides');var d=document.getElementById('dots');
    if(!s)return;
    s.innerHTML=this.banners.map(function(b){
      var img=b.img?'<img class="sl-img" src="'+esc(b.img)+'">':'';
      return '<div class="slide" style="background:'+esc(b.bg)+'">'+img+'<div class="sl-c"><div class="sl-tag">'+esc(b.tag||'')+'</div><div class="sl-title">'+esc(b.title||'')+'</div><div class="sl-sub">'+esc(b.sub||'')+'</div><button class="sl-btn">'+esc(b.btn||'تسوق')+'</button></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    d.innerHTML=this.banners.map(function(_,i){return '<div class="dot'+(i===0?' active':'')+'" onclick="APP.goSlide('+i+')"></div>';}).join('');
  },
  
  goSlide:function(i){
    var s=document.getElementById('slides');if(!s)return;
    s.style.transform='translateX('+(this.state.lang==='ar'?i*100:-i*100)+'%)';
    document.querySelectorAll('.dot').forEach(function(d,j){if(j===i)d.classList.add('active');else d.classList.remove('active');});
  },
  
  filterCat:function(k){
    if(k==='all'){this.renderProducts();return;}
    this.renderProducts(this.products.filter(function(p){return p.category===k;}));
  },
  
  search:function(q){q=q.trim().toLowerCase();if(!q){this.renderProducts();return;}this.renderProducts(this.products.filter(function(p){return (p.name||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1||(p.brand||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1;}));},
  
  openProduct:function(id){
    var p=null;this.products.forEach(function(x){if(String(x.id)===String(id))p=x;});
    if(!p)return;
    this.currentProd=p;this.currentQty=1;
    var ic=document.getElementById('det-img');
    var oi=ic.querySelector('img');if(oi)oi.remove();
    if(p.img){var img=document.createElement('img');img.src=p.img;ic.insertBefore(img,ic.firstChild);}
    document.getElementById('det-brand').textContent=p.brand||'';
    document.getElementById('det-name').textContent=p.name;
    document.getElementById('det-rate').innerHTML='⭐ '+esc(p.rating||4.5)+' ('+esc(p.reviews||0)+')';
    document.getElementById('det-price').innerHTML=esc(p.price)+' <span style="font-size:14px;">ر.س</span>'+(p.old?' <span style="font-size:14px;color:var(--mt2);text-decoration:line-through;">'+esc(p.old)+'</span>':'');
    document.getElementById('det-desc').textContent=p.desc||'';
    document.getElementById('det-qty').textContent='1';
    this.navigate('details',true);
  },
  
  chQty:function(d){this.currentQty=Math.max(1,this.currentQty+d);document.getElementById('det-qty').textContent=this.currentQty;},
  
  addDetCart:function(){if(!this.currentProd)return;this.addCart(this.currentProd.id,this.currentQty);this.toast('✓ تمت الإضافة');this.goTab('home');},
  buyNow:function(){if(!this.currentProd)return;this.addCart(this.currentProd.id,this.currentQty);this.goTab('cart');},
  
  addCart:function(id,qty){
    var ex=null;this.cart.forEach(function(i){if(String(i.id)===String(id))ex=i;});
    if(ex)ex.qty+=qty;else this.cart.push({id:id,qty:qty});
    this.saveCart();this.updateCartB();
  },
  
  saveCart:function(){try{localStorage.setItem('chizen_cart',JSON.stringify(this.cart));}catch(e){}},
  updateCartB:function(){var t=0;this.cart.forEach(function(i){t+=i.qty;});var b=document.getElementById('cart-b');if(b)b.textContent=t;},
  updateNotifB:function(){var b=document.getElementById('notif-b');if(b)b.textContent=this.notifications.length;},
  getProduct:function(id){for(var i=0;i<this.products.length;i++)if(String(this.products[i].id)===String(id))return this.products[i];return null;},
  
  chCartQty:function(id,d){
    for(var i=0;i<this.cart.length;i++){if(String(this.cart[i].id)===String(id)){this.cart[i].qty+=d;if(this.cart[i].qty<1)this.cart.splice(i,1);break;}}
    this.saveCart();this.updateCartB();this.renderCart();
  },
  removeCart:function(id){for(var i=0;i<this.cart.length;i++){if(String(this.cart[i].id)===String(id)){this.cart.splice(i,1);break;}}this.saveCart();this.updateCartB();this.renderCart();},
  
  cartTotal:function(){var t=0;this.cart.forEach(function(i){var p=APP.getProduct(i.id);if(p)t+=p.price*i.qty;});return t;},
  
  renderCart:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('cart-items');if(!c)return;
    if(!this.cart.length){c.innerHTML='<div class="cempty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="9" cy="21" r="1"/><circle cx="20" cy="21" r="1"/><path d="M1 1h4l2.68 13.39a2 2 0 0 0 2 1.61h9.72a2 2 0 0 0 2-1.61L23 6H6"/></svg><p>سلتك فارغة</p><button class="btn-p" style="margin-top:16px;max-width:200px;" onclick="APP.goTab(\'home\')">تسوق الآن</button></div>';return;}
    var sub=this.cartTotal();
    var disc=0;if(this.appliedCoupon){disc=this.appliedCoupon.type==='percent'?Math.floor(sub*(this.appliedCoupon.value/100)):this.appliedCoupon.value;}
    var shipF=0;if(sub>0&&(!this.shipping.freeAbove||sub<this.shipping.freeAbove))shipF=this.shipping.fee||0;
    var total=Math.max(0,sub-disc+shipF);
    var cp='';
    if(this.appliedCoupon)cp='<div class="tr" style="color:var(--gn);"><span>خصم ('+esc(this.appliedCoupon.code)+')</span><span>-' +disc+' ر.س <span style="cursor:pointer;color:var(--rd);" onclick="APP.rmCoupon()">✕</span></span></div>';
    else cp='<div class="f" style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:10px;"><input type="text" id="coupon-in" class="fi" placeholder="كود الخصم..." style="flex:1;"><button class="btn-p" style="width:auto;padding:0 20px;" onclick="APP.applyCoupon()">تطبيق</button></div>';
    var html=this.cart.map(function(i){var p=APP.getProduct(i.id);if(!p)return'';
      return '<div class="ci"><div class="ci-i"><img src="'+esc(p.img||'')+'"><div class="cr" onclick="APP.removeCart(\''+esc(p.id)+'\')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><line x1="18" y1="6" x2="6" y2="18"/><line x1="6" y1="6" x2="18" y2="18"/></svg></div></div><div class="ci-inf"><div class="ci-n">'+esc(p.name)+'</div><div class="ci-p">'+esc(p.price)+' ر.س</div><div class="qty"><button class="qb" onclick="APP.chCartQty(\''+esc(p.id)+'\',-1)">−</button><span class="qn">'+i.qty+'</span><button class="qb" onclick="APP.chCartQty(\''+esc(p.id)+'\',1)">+</button></div></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    html+='<div class="tb">'+cp+'<div class="tr"><span>المجموع</span><span>'+sub+' ر.س</span></div><div class="tr"><span>الشحن</span><span>'+(shipF===0?'مجاني':shipF+' ر.س')+'</span></div><div class="tf"><span>الإجمالي</span><span>'+total+' ر.س</span></div></div><button class="btn-p" style="margin-top:16px;" onclick="APP.goCheckout()">إتمام الشراء</button>';
    c.innerHTML=html;
  },
  
  applyCoupon:function(){
    var code=(document.getElementById('coupon-in').value||'').trim().toUpperCase();
    if(!code)return;
    var cp=null;this.coupons.forEach(function(c){if(c.code===code&&c.active)cp=c;});
    if(!cp){try{var local=localStorage.getItem('chizen_welcome_coupon');if(local&&local===code)cp={code:code,type:'percent',value:15,active:true};}catch(e){}}
    if(!cp){this.toast('كود غير صالح');return;}
    this.appliedCoupon=cp;this.toast('تم تطبيق الخصم ✓');this.renderCart();
  },
  rmCoupon:function(){this.appliedCoupon=null;this.renderCart();},
  
  goCheckout:function(){
    if(!this.cart.length)return;
    try{var s=localStorage.getItem('chizen_last_pickup');if(s&&!this.selectedPickup)this.selectPickup(s);}catch(e){}
    if(this.state.user){var n=document.getElementById('co-name');if(n)n.value=this.state.user.name||'';}
    var sub=this.cartTotal();
    document.getElementById('co-sub').textContent=sub+' ر.س';
    document.getElementById('co-total').textContent=sub+' ر.س';
    this.navigate('checkout',true);
  },
  
  renderBanks:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('bank-list');if(!c)return;
    c.innerHTML=this.banks.map(function(b){
      return '<div class="bank" onclick="APP.selectBank(\''+esc(b.id)+'\')"><div class="bank-i" style="background:'+esc(b.color)+'">'+esc(b.icon)+'</div><div style="flex:1;"><div class="bank-n">'+esc(b.name)+'</div><div class="bank-d">'+(b.account?esc(b.account):'ادفع عند الاستلام')+'</div></div><div class="bank-c"></div></div>';
    }).join('');
  },
  
  selectBank:function(id){
    this.selectedBank=id;
    document.querySelectorAll('.bank').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('selected');});
    document.querySelectorAll('.bank').forEach(function(b,i){if(APP.banks[i]&&APP.banks[i].id===id)b.classList.add('selected');});
  },
  
  confirmOrder:async function(){
    var pickup=this.selectedPickup||document.getElementById('pickup-val').value;
    if(!pickup){this.toast('اختر نقطة التسليم');this.openPickup();return;}
    var name=document.getElementById('co-name').value.trim();
    var phone=document.getElementById('co-phone').value.trim();
    if(!name||phone.length<8){this.toast('أدخل الاسم والهاتف');return;}
    if(!this.selectedBank){this.toast('اختر طريقة الدفع');return;}
    var oid='CH-'+Date.now().toString().slice(-8);
    var sub=this.cartTotal();
    var disc=0;if(this.appliedCoupon){disc=this.appliedCoupon.type==='percent'?Math.floor(sub*(this.appliedCoupon.value/100)):this.appliedCoupon.value;}
    var shipF=0;if(sub>0&&(!this.shipping.freeAbove||sub<this.shipping.freeAbove))shipF=this.shipping.fee||0;
    var total=Math.max(0,sub-disc+shipF);
    var order={id:oid,items:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.cart)),subtotal:sub,discount:disc,shipping:shipF,total:total,pickup:pickup,name:name,phone:phone,bank:this.selectedBank,status:'pending'};
    var self=this;
    if(window.FB&&FB.ready&&FB.addOrder){
      try{
        await FB.addOrder(order);
        if(FB.sendWhatsAppMessage){
          var waMsg = "أهلاً " + name + "، تم استلام طلبك رقم " + oid + " بنجاح.\nالإجمالي: " + total + " ريال.\nسنقوم بالتواصل معك قريباً لتأكيد التوصيل. شكراً لثقتك بـ CHIZEN!";
          FB.sendWhatsAppMessage(phone, waMsg);
        }
      }catch(e){}
    }
    this.orders.push(order);
    this.cart=[];this.appliedCoupon=null;this.saveCart();this.updateCartB();
    this.toast('✓ تم استلام طلبك');
    this.goTab('orders');
  },
  
  renderOrders:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('orders-list');if(!c)return;
    if(!this.orders.length){c.innerHTML='<div class="cempty"><p>لا توجد طلبات</p></div>';return;}
    var list=this.orders.slice().reverse();
    c.innerHTML=list.map(function(o){
      var ic=0;if(o.items)o.items.forEach(function(i){ic+=i.qty;});
      return '<div class="oc"><div class="oh"><div class="oi">#'+esc((o.id||'').substring(0,14))+'</div><div class="os '+(o.status||'pending')+'">'+(o.status==='pending'?'قيد المعالجة':o.status==='shipped'?'في الطريق':o.status==='completed'?'مكتمل':'ملغي')+'</div></div><div class="ob">'+ic+' منتج • 📍 '+esc(o.pickup||'')+'</div><div class="ot"><span>الإجمالي</span><span>'+esc(o.total)+' ر.س</span></div></div>';
    }).join('');
  },
  
  renderNotifs:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('notif-list');if(!c)return;
    if(!this.notifications.length){c.innerHTML='<div class="cempty"><p>لا توجد إشعارات</p></div>';return;}
    c.innerHTML=this.notifications.slice().reverse().map(function(n){
      return '<div class="oc"><div class="ob" style="color:var(--cr);font-weight:700;font-size:14px;">'+esc(n.title)+'</div><div class="ob" style="margin-top:6px;">'+esc(n.body)+'</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  },
  
  renderFav:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('fav-list');if(!c)return;
    if(!this.favorites.length){c.innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--mt);">لا توجد منتجات في المفضلة</div>';return;}
    var list=[];this.favorites.forEach(function(id){var p=APP.getProduct(id);if(p)list.push(p);});
    this.renderProducts(list);
    document.getElementById('fav-list').innerHTML=document.getElementById('prods').innerHTML;
  },
  
  togFav:function(id,el){
    var idx=this.favorites.indexOf(id);
    if(idx>-1){this.favorites.splice(idx,1);if(el)el.classList.remove('active');}
    else{this.favorites.push(id);if(el)el.classList.add('active');}
    try{localStorage.setItem('chizen_favorites',JSON.stringify(this.favorites));}catch(e){}
  },
  
  renderPoints:function(){
    var p=this.state.loyaltyPoints||0;
    var n=document.getElementById('pt-num');if(n)n.textContent=p;
    var v=document.getElementById('pt-val');if(v)v.textContent='= '+Math.floor(p/10)+' ر.س خصم';
    var tx=this.getPointsTx();
    var c=document.getElementById('pt-list');
    if(c)c.innerHTML=tx.length?tx.slice().reverse().map(function(t){return '<div class="pt-t"><div class="pt-ti">'+(t.amount>0?'⬆️':'⬇️')+'</div><div class="pt-tinf"><div class="pt-tt">'+esc(t.title)+'</div></div><div class="pt-ta">'+(t.amount>0?'+':'')+esc(t.amount)+'</div></div>';}).join(''):'<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--mt);">لا توجد حركات</div>';
  },
  getPointsTx:function(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_points_tx')||'[]');}catch(e){return[];}},
  addPointsTx:function(title,amount){var tx=this.getPointsTx();tx.push({title:title,amount:amount,date:new Date().toISOString()});if(tx.length>100)tx.shift();try{localStorage.setItem('chizen_points_tx',JSON.stringify(tx));}catch(e){}},
  
  dailyCheckin:function(){
    var last=localStorage.getItem('chizen_last_checkin');
    var today=new Date().toDateString();
    if(last===today){this.toast('سجلت اليوم');return;}
    localStorage.setItem('chizen_last_checkin',today);
    this.state.loyaltyPoints=(this.state.loyaltyPoints||0)+10;
    this.updateLoyalty();this.addPointsTx('تسجيل يومي',10);
    if(window.FB&&FB.ready&&FB.updateUserPoints)FB.updateUserPoints(this.state.loyaltyPoints).catch(function(){});
    this.renderPoints();this.toast('+10 نقطة 🎁');
  },
  sharePoints:function(){if(navigator.share)navigator.share({title:'CHIZEN',text:'جرب CHIZEN!'}).catch(function(){});else this.toast('المشاركة غير مدعومة');},
  reviewPoints:function(){
    var self=this;
    this.showPrompt('قيّم من 1 إلى 5','5',function(r){
      if(!r)return;var n=parseInt(r);if(n<1||n>5){self.toast('من 1 إلى 5');return;}
      self.state.loyaltyPoints=(self.state.loyaltyPoints||0)+50;
      self.updateLoyalty();self.addPointsTx('تقييم',50);self.renderPoints();
      if(window.FB&&FB.ready&&FB.updateUserPoints)FB.updateUserPoints(self.state.loyaltyPoints).catch(function(){});
      self.toast('+50 نقطة ⭐');
    });
  },
  
  renderHelp:function(){
    var c=document.getElementById('help-c');if(!c)return;
    var ct={phone:'+967 77 123 4567',whatsapp:'967771234567',email:'support@chizen.com'};
    try{var s=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_contact')||'{}');Object.assign(ct,s);}catch(e){}
    c.innerHTML='<div class="sit" onclick="window.location.href=\'tel:'+esc(ct.phone)+'\'" style="background:var(--nav);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:10px;"><div class="sii"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M22 16.92v3a2 2 0 0 1-2.18 2 19.79 19.79 0 0 1-8.63-3.07"/></svg></div><div class="sit-t"><div class="sit-ti">اتصل بنا</div><div class="sit-d">'+esc(ct.phone)+'</div></div></div><div class="sit" onclick="window.open(\'https://wa.me/'+esc(ct.whatsapp)+'\',\'_blank\')" style="background:var(--nav);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:10px;"><div class="sii">💬</div><div class="sit-t"><div class="sit-ti">واتساب</div></div></div><div class="sit" style="background:var(--nav);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;"><div class="sii">✉️</div><div class="sit-t"><div class="sit-ti">البريد</div><div class="sit-d">'+esc(ct.email)+'</div></div></div>';
  },
  
  renderSettings:function(){
    document.getElementById('v-lang').textContent=this.state.lang==='ar'?'العربية':'English';
    document.getElementById('v-theme').textContent=this.state.theme==='dark'?'داكن':'فاتح';
    document.getElementById('v-font').textContent=this.state.fontSize==='small'?'صغير':this.state.fontSize==='large'?'كبير':'متوسط';
    this.setTog('t-notif',this.state.notifications);this.setTog('t-offers',this.state.offers);this.setTog('t-orders',this.state.orderNotifs);this.setTog('t-bio',this.state.biometric);
    var size=0;try{for(var k in localStorage)if(localStorage.hasOwnProperty(k))size+=localStorage[k].length;}catch(e){}
    document.getElementById('cache-s').textContent=Math.round(size/1024)+' KB';
  },
  setTog:function(id,v){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;if(v)el.classList.add('on');else el.classList.remove('on');},
  togT:function(k,el){this.state[k]=!this.state[k];if(this.state[k])el.classList.add('on');else el.classList.remove('on');this.saveState();this.toast('تم الحفظ ✓');},
  
  openLang:function(){var self=this;this.openModal('اللغة',[{v:'ar',l:'العربية'},{v:'en',l:'English'}],this.state.lang,function(v){self.state.lang=v;self.applyState();self.saveState();self.renderSettings();self.toast('✓');});},
  openTheme:function(){var self=this;this.openModal('الوضع',[{v:'dark',l:'داكن'},{v:'light',l:'فاتح'}],this.state.theme,function(v){self.state.theme=v;self.applyState();self.saveState();self.renderSettings();self.toast('✓');});},
  openFont:function(){var self=this;this.openModal('حجم الخط',[{v:'small',l:'صغير'},{v:'medium',l:'متوسط'},{v:'large',l:'كبير'}],this.state.fontSize,function(v){self.state.fontSize=v;self.saveState();self.renderSettings();self.toast('✓');});},
  openModal:function(title,opts,cur,cb){
    document.getElementById('modal-t').textContent=title;
    document.getElementById('modal-c').innerHTML=opts.map(function(o){return '<div class="mo'+(o.v===cur?' selected':'')+'" onclick="APP.selectModal(\''+o.v+'\')">'+o.l+'</div>';}).join('');
    window._modalCb=cb;
    document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('active');
  },
  selectModal:function(v){if(window._modalCb)window._modalCb(v);this.closeModal();},
  
  togBio:function(){this.state.biometric=!this.state.biometric;this.saveState();this.renderSettings();this.toast(this.state.biometric?'✓ مفعل':'موقوف');},
  
  clearCache:function(){
    var self=this;
    this.showConfirm('مسح الكاش؟','لا يؤثر على بياناتك',async function(ok){
      if(!ok)return;
      try{if('caches' in window){var keys=await caches.keys();keys.forEach(function(k){caches.delete(k);});}}catch(e){}
      var keep=['chizen_state','chizen_cart','chizen_favorites','chizen_orders','chizen_points_tx','chizen_last_pickup','chizen_welcome_coupon','chizen_contact'];
      for(var k in localStorage)if(localStorage.hasOwnProperty(k)&&keep.indexOf(k)===-1)localStorage.removeItem(k);
      self.toast('تم ✓');self.renderSettings();
    });
  },
  
  backup:function(){
    var data={};try{['chizen_state','chizen_cart','chizen_favorites','chizen_orders','chizen_points_tx'].forEach(function(k){var v=localStorage.getItem(k);if(v)data[k]=v;});}catch(e){}
    try{var blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});var url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);var a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='chizen-backup-'+Date.now()+'.json';a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);this.toast('✓');}catch(e){this.toast('فشل');}
  },
  
  changePass:function(){
    var self=this;
    this.showPrompt('كلمة المرور الحالية','',function(oldP){
      if(!oldP)return;
      if(window.FB&&FB.ready&&FB.currentUser){
        try{var cred=firebase.auth.EmailAuthProvider.credential(FB.currentUser.email,oldP);
          FB.currentUser.reauthenticateWithCredential(cred).then(function(){
            self.showPrompt('كلمة المرور الجديدة (6+)','',function(np){if(!np||np.length<6){self.toast('قصيرة');return;}FB.currentUser.updatePassword(np).then(function(){self.toast('✓');}).catch(function(){self.toast('فشل');});},{type:'password'});
          }).catch(function(){self.toast('كلمة المرور خاطئة');});
        }catch(e){self.toast('فشل');}
      }else{self.toast('غير مدعوم');}
    },{type:'password'});
  },
  
  editProfile:function(){
    var self=this;var u=this.state.user||{name:'User'};
    this.showPrompt('الاسم الكامل',u.name,function(name){if(!name||name.length<2){self.toast('قصير');return;}self.state.user.name=name;self.saveState();self.updateUserUI();if(window.FB&&FB.ready)try{FB.updateDisplayName(name);FB.saveUserProfile({name:name});}catch(e){}self.toast('✓');});
  },
  
  deleteAccount:function(){
    var self=this;
    this.showConfirm('حذف الحساب نهائياً؟','⚠️ لا يمكن الرجوع',function(ok){
      if(!ok)return;
      self.showConfirm('تأكيد أخير','هل أنت متأكد؟',async function(ok2){
        if(!ok2)return;
        try{if(window.FB&&FB.ready&&FB.currentUser){try{await FB.db.collection('users').doc(FB.currentUser.uid).delete();}catch(e){}try{await FB.currentUser.delete();}catch(e){}}var keys=['chizen_state','chizen_cart','chizen_orders','chizen_favorites','chizen_points_tx'];keys.forEach(function(k){localStorage.removeItem(k);});}catch(e){}
        window.location.replace('index.html');
      });
    });
  },
  
  logout:function(){
    var self=this;
    this.showConfirm('تسجيل الخروج؟','',async function(ok){
      if(!ok)return;
      try{if(window.FB&&FB.ready)await FB.logout();}catch(e){}
      localStorage.removeItem('chizen_state');
      window.location.replace('index.html');
    });
  },
  
  openPickup:function(){
    var self=this;
    if(this.pickupLocations){this.renderPickup();return;}
    if(window.FB&&FB.ready){FB.getConfig('delivery_locations').then(function(d){self.pickupLocations=d||self.getFallback();self.renderPickup();}).catch(function(){self.pickupLocations=self.getFallback();self.renderPickup();});}
    else{this.pickupLocations=this.getFallback();this.renderPickup();}
  },
  getFallback:function(){return{aden:[{district:'المعلا',areas:['الشارع الرئيسي','سوق المعلا']},{district:'كريتر',areas:['شارع الملكة أروى']}],yemen:[{gov:'تعز',station:'مكتب الحوبان'}]};},
  renderPickup:function(){
    var data=this.pickupLocations;var self=this;
    if(!data)return;
    var tabs='<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:16px;"><button onclick="APP.switchPickup(\'aden\')" style="flex:1;padding:12px;background:'+(self.currentPickupTab==='aden'?'var(--grad2)':'rgba(2,8,35,.5)')+';border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;color:#fff;font-family:Tajawal,sans-serif;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">🏙️ داخل عدن</button><button onclick="APP.switchPickup(\'yemen\')" style="flex:1;padding:12px;background:'+(self.currentPickupTab==='yemen'?'var(--grad2)':'rgba(2,8,35,.5)')+';border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;color:#fff;font-family:Tajawal,sans-serif;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">🚌 خارج عدن</button></div>';
    var content='';
    if(self.currentPickupTab==='aden'){
      if(self.currentDistrict){
        var dist=data.aden.find(function(d){return d.district===self.currentDistrict;});
        content+='<button onclick="APP.currentDistrict=null;APP.renderPickup();" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--pk);font-family:Tajawal,sans-serif;font-weight:900;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;padding:8px 0;margin-bottom:12px;">← رجوع</button>';
        if(dist&&dist.areas){content+=dist.areas.map(function(a){return '<div style="padding:14px;background:rgba(2,8,35,.5);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.selectPickup(\''+esc(dist.district)+' - '+esc(a)+'\')"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;"><span style="color:var(--cr);font-weight:700;">'+esc(a)+'</span><span style="color:var(--pk);">اختيار ›</span></div></div>';}).join('');}
      }else{
        content+='<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;">';
        (data.aden||[]).forEach(function(d){content+='<div style="padding:16px;background:rgba(2,8,35,.5);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.currentDistrict=\''+esc(d.district)+'\';APP.renderPickup()"><div style="font-size:22px;margin-bottom:6px;">🏘️</div><div style="color:var(--cr);font-weight:900;font-size:13px;">'+esc(d.district)+'</div></div>';});
        content+='</div>';
      }
    }else{
      content+=(data.yemen||[]).map(function(g){return '<div style="padding:14px;background:rgba(2,8,35,.5);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:8px;cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.selectPickup(\''+esc(g.gov)+' - '+esc(g.station)+'\')"><div style="color:var(--cr);font-weight:900;">'+esc(g.gov)+'</div><div style="color:var(--mt);font-size:12px;margin-top:4px;">'+esc(g.station)+'</div></div>';}).join('');
    }
    document.getElementById('modal-t').textContent='📍 أين تريد استلام طلبك؟';
    document.getElementById('modal-c').innerHTML=tabs+content;
    document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('active');
  },
  switchPickup:function(t){this.currentPickupTab=t;this.currentDistrict=null;this.renderPickup();},
  selectPickup:function(v){
    this.selectedPickup=v;
    document.getElementById('pickup-val').value=v;
    var btn=document.getElementById('pickup-txt');if(btn){btn.textContent='📍 '+v;btn.style.color='var(--gn)';}
    try{localStorage.setItem('chizen_last_pickup',v);}catch(e){}
    this.closeModal();this.toast('✓ تم اختيار النقطة');
  },
  
  showPrompt:function(title,def,cb,opts){
    opts=opts||{};var box=document.getElementById('dlb');
    box.innerHTML='<div class="dlt">'+esc(title)+'</div><input type="'+(opts.type||'text')+'" id="dl-in" class="dli" value="'+esc(def||'')+'" placeholder="'+(opts.placeholder||'')+'"><div class="dla"><button class="dlb2 cancel" onclick="APP.closeDlg()">إلغاء</button><button class="dlb2 primary" onclick="APP.subPrompt()">موافق</button></div>';
    window._promptCb=cb;
    document.getElementById('dlg').classList.add('active');
    setTimeout(function(){var i=document.getElementById('dl-in');if(i){i.focus();i.select();}},100);
  },
  subPrompt:function(){var i=document.getElementById('dl-in');var v=i?i.value:'';this.closeDlg();if(window._promptCb)window._promptCb(v);},
  showConfirm:function(title,msg,cb){
    var box=document.getElementById('dlb');
    box.innerHTML='<div class="dlt">'+esc(title)+'</div>'+(msg?'<div class="dlm">'+esc(msg)+'</div>':'')+'<div class="dla"><button class="dlb2 cancel" onclick="APP.closeDlg()">إلغاء</button><button class="dlb2 danger" onclick="APP.subConfirm()">تأكيد</button></div>';
    window._confirmCb=cb;
    document.getElementById('dlg').classList.add('active');
  },
  subConfirm:function(){this.closeDlg();if(window._confirmCb)window._confirmCb(true);},
  
  toast:function(msg){var t=document.getElementById('toast');if(!t)return;t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(window._tt);window._tt=setTimeout(function(){t.classList.remove('show');},2500);}
};

if('serviceWorker' in navigator){window.addEventListener('load',function(){navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(function(){});});}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){APP.init();});
else APP.init();
</script>

<style>
#ptr-refresh { position: fixed; top: -60px; left: 0; right: 0; height: 60px; display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; background: #1F0D35; color: #ff27c9; font-family: Tajawal, sans-serif; font-weight: bold; z-index: 100000; transition: top 0.3s ease; }

<div id="ptr-refresh">🔄 جاري التحديث...</div>


<script>
let touchStartY = 0;
let isPulling = false;
document.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
    if (window.scrollY <= 0) {
        touchStartY = e.touches[0].pageY;
        isPulling = true;
    }
}, { passive: true });
document.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
    if (!isPulling) return;
    let currentY = e.touches[0].pageY;
    if (currentY - touchStartY > 100 && window.scrollY <= 0) {
        document.getElementById('ptr-refresh').classList.add('active');
    }
}, { passive: true });
document.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
    if (!isPulling) return;
    isPulling = false;
    let ptr = document.getElementById('ptr-refresh');
    if (ptr && ptr.classList.contains('active')) {
        setTimeout(function() {
            location.reload();
            ptr.classList.remove('active');
        }, 1000);
    }
});
</script>

<script>
window.renderCategoryProducts = function(catKey, catName) {
    // إخفاء كل الأقسام وإظهار قسم المنتجات
    var sec = document.getElementById('tab-home');
    if(!sec) return;
    var grid = document.getElementById('products-grid') || document.querySelector('.products-grid');
    if(!grid) return;

    // إظهار لودر
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:50px;color:#fff;">جاري تحميل المنتجات...</div>';

    // سحب المنتجات من Firebase
    if(window.FB && FB.getProducts) {
        FB.getProducts().then(function(prods) {
            var filtered = prods.filter(function(p) { return p.category === catKey; });
            if(filtered.length === 0) {
                grid.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:50px;color:#fff;">لا توجد منتجات في هذا القسم حالياً</div>';
            } else {
                grid.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(p) {
                    return '<div class="p-card" onclick="App.openProduct(\''+p.id+'\')">' +
                           '<img src="'+(p.image||'')+'" onerror="this.src=\'https://via.placeholder.com/150\'">' +
                           '<div class="p-name">'+p.name+'</div>' +
                           '<div class="p-price">'+p.price+' ر.س</div></div>';
                }).join('');
            }
            // تحديث عنوان الصفحة
            var h = document.querySelector('.page-title');
            if(h) h.innerText = catName;
        });
    }
};
</script>

var COUNTRIES = [{ code:'+967', flag:'🇾🇪', name:'اليمن', len:9 },{ code:'+966', flag:'🇸🇦', name:'السعودية', len:9 },{ code:'+971', flag:'🇦🇪', name:'الإمارات', len:9 },{ code:'+965', flag:'🇰🇼', name:'الكويت', len:8 },{ code:'+974', flag:'🇶🇦', name:'قطر', len:8 },{ code:'+973', flag:'🇧🇭', name:'البحرين', len:8 },{ code:'+968', flag:'🇴🇲', name:'عُمان', len:8 },{ code:'+962', flag:'🇯🇴', name:'الأردن', len:9 },{ code:'+20',  flag:'🇪🇬', name:'مصر', len:10 },{ code:'+964', flag:'🇮🇶', name:'العراق', len:10 },{ code:'+963', flag:'🇸🇾', name:'سوريا', len:9 },{ code:'+961', flag:'🇱🇧', name:'لبنان', len:8 },{ code:'+970', flag:'🇵🇸', name:'فلسطين', len:9 },{ code:'+212', flag:'🇲🇦', name:'المغرب', len:9 },{ code:'+213', flag:'🇩🇿', name:'الجزائر', len:9 },{ code:'+216', flag:'🇹🇳', name:'تونس', len:8 }];
function withTimeout(promise, ms, fallback) { return Promise.race([ promise, new Promise(function(resolve){ setTimeout(function(){ resolve(fallback !== undefined ? fallback : null); }, ms); }) ]); }
var AUTH = {
  _busy: false, _busySince: 0, _fbReady: false, _redirecting: false, _selectedCountry: COUNTRIES[0], _phoneMethod: 'sms', _confirmationResult: null, _recaptchaVerifier: null, _phoneNumber: null, _otpTimer: null, _resendCooldown: 0,
  _setBusy: function(v) { this._busy = v; this._busySince = v ? Date.now() : 0; },
  redirect: function(url) { if (this._redirecting) return; this._redirecting = true; window.location.replace(url || 'chizen.html'); },
  init: function() {
    var self = this;
    this.showScreen('login');
    try {
      var rem = localStorage.getItem('chizen_remember_email');
      if (rem) { document.getElementById('login-email').value = rem; document.getElementById('login-remember').checked = true; }
      var savedCountry = localStorage.getItem('chizen_country');
      if (savedCountry) { var found = COUNTRIES.find(function(c){ return c.code === savedCountry; }); if (found) { this._selectedCountry = found; this.updateCountryUI(); } }
    } catch(e) {}
    this.bindPwStrength('signup-password', 'signup-strength', 'signup-strength-label');
    this.bindErrorClear();
    this.renderCountries();
    this.initOTPInputs();
    window.addEventListener('offline', function(){ document.getElementById('offline-banner').classList.add('show'); });
    window.addEventListener('online',  function(){ document.getElementById('offline-banner').classList.remove('show'); });
    setInterval(function() {
      if (self._busy && self._busySince > 0 && (Date.now() - self._busySince) > 30000) {
        self._setBusy(false);
        ['btn-login','btn-signup','btn-forgot','btn-google','btn-facebook','btn-send-otp','btn-verify-otp','btn-resend'].forEach(function(id){ self.setLoading(id, false); });
        self.hideOverlay(); self.showErr('login-err', 'انتهت المهلة — حاول مرة أخرى');
      }
    }, 5000);
    this._fbReady = !!(window.FB && window.FB.ready);
    if (!this._fbReady) { this.waitForFB(3000).then(function(ready){ self._fbReady = ready; if (ready) self.afterFBReady(); }); }
    else { this.afterFBReady(); }
  },
  afterFBReady: function() {
    var self = this;
    withTimeout((async function(){
      try {
        var redirectUser = await FB.handleGoogleRedirect();
        if (redirectUser) {
          var existing = await FB.getUserProfile();
          if (!existing) { await FB.saveUserProfile({ name: redirectUser.displayName || 'مستخدم', email: redirectUser.email || '', phone: '', loyaltyPoints: 240, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); }
          self.redirect(); return true;
        }
      } catch(e) { console.warn('Redirect check failed:', e); }
      return false;
    })(), 4000, false).then(function(redirected){
      if (redirected) return;
      try { FB.onAuthChange(function(user) { if (user && !self._redirecting) { self.redirect(); } }); } catch(e) { console.warn('onAuthChange failed:', e); }
    });
  },
  waitForFB: function(timeout) { return new Promise(function(resolve) { var start = Date.now(); (function check() { if (window.FB && window.FB.ready) return resolve(true); if (Date.now() - start > timeout) return resolve(false); setTimeout(check, 100); })(); }); },
  showScreen: function(id) {
    var s = document.querySelectorAll('.screen'); for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) s[i].classList.remove('active');
    var t = document.getElementById('screen-' + id); if (t) { t.classList.add('active'); t.scrollTop = 0; }
    ['login-err','signup-err','forgot-err','phone-err','otp-err'].forEach(function(x){ var el = document.getElementById(x); if (el) el.classList.remove('show'); });
    if (id !== 'otp') clearInterval(this._otpTimer);
  },
  isEmail: function(v){ return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v); },
  isPhone: function(v){ var d = (v||'').replace(/\D/g,''); return d.length >= 7 && d.length <= 15; },
  pwStrength: function(p){ if (!p) return 0; var s = 0; if (p.length >= 6) s++; if (p.length >= 10) s++; if (/[A-Z]/.test(p) && /[a-z]/.test(p)) s++; if (/\d/.test(p)) s++; if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(p)) s++; return Math.min(s, 4); },
  setLoading: function(btnId, loading) {
    var btn = document.getElementById(btnId); if (!btn) return;
    if (loading) { btn.disabled = true; if (!btn.dataset.original) btn.dataset.original = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-sm"></div><span>جاري...</span>'; }
    else { btn.disabled = false; if (btn.dataset.original) btn.innerHTML = btn.dataset.original; }
  },
  showOverlay: function(text) { document.getElementById('load-text').textContent = text || 'جاري المعالجة...'; document.getElementById('load-overlay').classList.add('show'); },
  hideOverlay: function() { document.getElementById('load-overlay').classList.remove('show'); },
  showErr: function(id, msg) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (!el) return; el.textContent = msg; el.classList.add('show'); clearTimeout(el._timer); el._timer = setTimeout(function(){ el.classList.remove('show'); }, 6000); },
  clearErr: function(id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.classList.remove('show'); },
  bindPwStrength: function(inputId, wrapId, labelId) {
    var inp = document.getElementById(inputId); var wrap = document.getElementById(wrapId); var label = document.getElementById(labelId);
    if (!inp || !wrap || !label) return;
    var bars = wrap.querySelectorAll('.pw-bar'); var texts = ['', 'ضعيفة', 'متوسطة', 'قوية', 'قوية جداً'];
    inp.addEventListener('input', function() {
      var v = inp.value; if (!v) { wrap.classList.remove('show'); return; }
      wrap.classList.add('show'); var score = AUTH.pwStrength(v);
      bars.forEach(function(b, i){ b.className = 'pw-bar' + (i < score ? ' s' + score : ''); });
      label.textContent = texts[score] || 'ضعيفة'; label.className = 'pw-label ' + (score >= 3 ? 'good' : score <= 1 ? 'bad' : '');
    });
  },
  bindErrorClear: function() {
    var pairs = [['login-email','login-err'],['login-password','login-err'],['signup-name','signup-err'],['signup-email','signup-err'],['signup-phone','signup-err'],['signup-password','signup-err'],['signup-confirm','signup-err'],['forgot-email','forgot-err'],['phone-number','phone-err']];
    pairs.forEach(function(p){ var inp = document.getElementById(p[0]); if (inp) inp.addEventListener('input', function(){ AUTH.clearErr(p[1]); }); });
  },
  renderCountries: function() {
    var dd = document.getElementById('country-dropdown'); if (!dd) return;
    dd.innerHTML = COUNTRIES.map(function(c) { return '<div class="country-option" data-code="'+c.code+'" onclick="AUTH.selectCountry(\''+c.code+'\')"><span class="flag">'+c.flag+'</span><span class="name">'+c.name+'</span><span class="code">'+c.code+'</span></div>'; }).join('');
  },
  toggleCountryDropdown: function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); var dd = document.getElementById('country-dropdown'); if (dd) dd.classList.toggle('show'); },
  selectCountry: function(code) {
    var c = COUNTRIES.find(function(x){ return x.code === code; }); if (!c) return;
    this._selectedCountry = c; this.updateCountryUI(); var dd = document.getElementById('country-dropdown'); if (dd) dd.classList.remove('show');
    try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_country', code); } catch(e) {}
    var input = document.getElementById('phone-number'); if (input) { input.maxLength = c.len; input.focus(); }
  },
  updateCountryUI: function() {
    var f = document.getElementById('country-flag'); var c = document.getElementById('country-code');
    if (f) f.textContent = this._selectedCountry.flag; if (c) c.textContent = this._selectedCountry.code;
    var input = document.getElementById('phone-number'); if (input) input.maxLength = this._selectedCountry.len || 15;
  },
  setMethod: function(method, btn) { this._phoneMethod = method; document.querySelectorAll('.method-btn').forEach(function(b){ b.classList.remove('active'); }); if (btn) btn.classList.add('active'); },
  initOTPInputs: function() {
    var inputs = document.querySelectorAll('.otp-input'); var self = this;
    inputs.forEach(function(input, i) {
      input.addEventListener('input', function(e) {
        var val = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ''); e.target.value = val.slice(0, 1);
        if (val) { e.target.classList.add('filled'); if (i < inputs.length - 1) inputs[i + 1].focus(); } else { e.target.classList.remove('filled'); }
        self.clearOTPError();
      });
      input.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !e.target.value && i > 0) { inputs[i-1].focus(); inputs[i-1].value = ''; inputs[i-1].classList.remove('filled'); }
        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && i < inputs.length - 1) { e.preventDefault(); inputs[i+1].focus(); }
        if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && i > 0) { e.preventDefault(); inputs[i-1].focus(); }
        if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); AUTH.verifyOTP(); }
      });
      input.addEventListener('paste', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault(); var paste = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData('text').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6);
        for (var j = 0; j < 6; j++) { inputs[j].value = paste[j] || ''; if (paste[j]) inputs[j].classList.add('filled'); else inputs[j].classList.remove('filled'); }
        var next = Math.min(paste.length, 5); inputs[next].focus();
      });
      input.addEventListener('focus', function(){ this.select(); });
    });
  },
  getOTPValue: function() { var code = ''; document.querySelectorAll('.otp-input').forEach(function(inp){ code += inp.value; }); return code; },
  clearOTP: function() { document.querySelectorAll('.otp-input').forEach(function(inp){ inp.value = ''; inp.classList.remove('filled','error'); }); },
  clearOTPError: function() { document.querySelectorAll('.otp-input').forEach(function(inp){ inp.classList.remove('error'); }); this.clearErr('otp-err'); },
  sendPhoneOTP: async function() {
    if (this._busy) return;
    var raw = (document.getElementById('phone-number').value || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); var errId = 'phone-err';
    if (!raw || raw.length < 7) { this.showErr(errId, 'أدخل رقم جوال صحيح'); return; }
    if (raw.length < (this._selectedCountry.len - 2)) { this.showErr(errId, 'الرقم قصير جداً'); return; }
    if (!this._fbReady) { this.showErr(errId, 'جاري التحميل — حاول بعد لحظات'); return; }
    var clean = raw.replace(/^0+/, ''); var fullPhone = this._selectedCountry.code + clean;
    this._setBusy(true); this.setLoading('btn-send-otp', true);
    try {
      if (this._recaptchaVerifier) { try { this._recaptchaVerifier.clear(); } catch(e) {} this._recaptchaVerifier = null; }
      this._recaptchaVerifier = new firebase.auth.RecaptchaVerifier('recaptcha-container', { size: 'invisible', callback: function(){} });
      var result = await withTimeout(FB.auth.signInWithPhoneNumber(fullPhone, this._recaptchaVerifier), 20000, null);
      if (!result) throw new Error('timeout');
      this._confirmationResult = result; this._phoneNumber = fullPhone;
      document.getElementById('otp-phone-display').textContent = this.formatPhoneDisplay(fullPhone);
      this.setLoading('btn-send-otp', false); this._setBusy(false);
      this.clearOTP(); this.showScreen('otp'); this.startResendTimer();
      var msg = this._phoneMethod === 'whatsapp' ? 'تم إرسال الرمز عبر واتساب ✓' : 'تم إرسال الرمز عبر رسالة نصية ✓';
      this.toast(msg, 'success');
      setTimeout(function(){ var first = document.querySelector('.otp-input'); if (first) first.focus(); }, 350);
    } catch(e) {
      this.setLoading('btn-send-otp', false); this._setBusy(false);
      var msg = 'فشل الإرسال'; var c = e.code || '';
      if (e.message === 'timeout') msg = 'انتهت المهلة — حاول مجدداً';
      else if (c === 'auth/invalid-phone-number') msg = 'رقم الجوال غير صحيح';
      else if (c === 'auth/too-many-requests') msg = 'محاولات كثيرة — حاول لاحقاً';
      else if (c === 'auth/quota-exceeded') msg = 'تم تجاوز الحد اليومي';
      else if (c === 'auth/captcha-check-failed') msg = 'فشل التحقق — أعد المحاولة';
      else if (c === 'auth/network-request-failed') msg = 'تحقق من الإنترنت';
      this.showErr(errId, msg);
    }
  },
  formatPhoneDisplay: function(phone) { if (!phone) return ''; var cc = this._selectedCountry.code; var rest = phone.slice(cc.length); var masked = rest.length > 4 ? rest.slice(0, 3) + ' XXX ' + rest.slice(-2) : rest; return cc + ' ' + masked; },
  startResendTimer: function() {
    var self = this; clearInterval(this._otpTimer); this._resendCooldown = 60;
    var btn = document.getElementById('btn-resend'); var timer = document.getElementById('resend-timer');
    if (!btn || !timer) return; btn.disabled = true; timer.classList.add('show'); timer.textContent = '(' + this._resendCooldown + 's)';
    this._otpTimer = setInterval(function(){ self._resendCooldown--; if (self._resendCooldown <= 0) { clearInterval(self._otpTimer); btn.disabled = false; timer.classList.remove('show'); } else { timer.textContent = '(' + self._resendCooldown + 's)'; } }, 1000);
  },
  resendOTP: async function() {
    if (this._resendCooldown > 0 || this._busy) return;
    if (!this._phoneNumber) { this.showErr('otp-err', 'أعد إدخال الرقم أولاً'); return; }
    this._setBusy(true); var btn = document.getElementById('btn-resend'); var orig = btn.textContent; btn.textContent = 'جاري...'; btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      if (this._recaptchaVerifier) { try { this._recaptchaVerifier.clear(); } catch(e) {} this._recaptchaVerifier = null; }
      this._recaptchaVerifier = new firebase.auth.RecaptchaVerifier('recaptcha-container', { size: 'invisible', callback: function(){} });
      var result = await withTimeout(FB.auth.signInWithPhoneNumber(this._phoneNumber, this._recaptchaVerifier), 20000, null);
      if (!result) throw new Error('timeout');
      this._confirmationResult = result; btn.textContent = orig; this._setBusy(false); this.toast('تم إعادة إرسال الرمز ✓', 'success'); this.startResendTimer();
    } catch(e) { btn.textContent = orig; btn.disabled = false; this._setBusy(false); var msg = 'فشل الإرسال'; if (e.code === 'auth/too-many-requests') msg = 'انتظر قليلاً قبل إعادة المحاولة'; this.showErr('otp-err', msg); }
  },
  verifyOTP: async function() {
    if (this._busy) return; var code = this.getOTPValue();
    if (code.length !== 6) { this.showErr('otp-err', 'أدخل الرمز المكوّن من 6 أرقام'); return; }
    if (!this._confirmationResult) { this.showErr('otp-err', 'أعد إرسال الرمز أولاً'); return; }
    this._setBusy(true); this.setLoading('btn-verify-otp', true); this.clearOTPError();
    try {
      var result = await withTimeout(this._confirmationResult.confirm(code), 15000, null); if (!result) throw new Error('timeout');
      var user = result.user; var existing = null;
      try { existing = await withTimeout(FB.getUserProfile(), 5000, null); } catch(e) {}
      if (!existing) { await withTimeout(FB.saveUserProfile({ name: user.displayName || 'مستخدم', email: user.email || '', phone: user.phoneNumber || this._phoneNumber, phoneVerified: true, loyaltyPoints: 240, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }), 8000, null); }
      this.redirect();
    } catch(e) {
      this.setLoading('btn-verify-otp', false); this._setBusy(false);
      var msg = 'الرمز غير صحيح'; var c = e.code || '';
      if (e.message === 'timeout') msg = 'انتهت المهلة — حاول مجدداً';
      else if (c === 'auth/invalid-verification-code') msg = 'الرمز غير صحيح — حاول مجدداً';
      else if (c === 'auth/code-expired') msg = 'انتهت صلاحية الرمز — اطلب رمزاً جديداً';
      else if (c === 'auth/too-many-requests') msg = 'محاولات كثيرة — حاول لاحقاً';
      this.showErr('otp-err', msg); document.querySelectorAll('.otp-input').forEach(function(inp){ inp.classList.add('error'); });
      setTimeout(function(){ document.querySelectorAll('.otp-input').forEach(function(inp){ inp.classList.remove('error'); }); }, 1500);
    }
  },
  login: async function() {
    if (this._busy) return;
    var email = (document.getElementById('login-email').value || '').trim().toLowerCase(); var pass = document.getElementById('login-password').value || ''; var remember = document.getElementById('login-remember').checked;
    if (!this.isEmail(email)) { this.showErr('login-err', 'أدخل بريداً صحيحاً'); return; }
    if (pass.length < 6) { this.showErr('login-err', 'كلمة المرور 6 أحرف على الأقل'); return; }
    /* تم إزالة فحص _fbReady — Firebase جاهز دائماً */
    this._setBusy(true); this.setLoading('btn-login', true);
    try {
      await withTimeout(FB.loginEmail(email, pass), 20000, null);
      try { if (remember) localStorage.setItem('chizen_remember_email', email); else localStorage.removeItem('chizen_remember_email'); } catch(e) {}
      this.redirect();
    } catch (e) {
      this.setLoading('btn-login', false); this._setBusy(false);
      var msg = 'حدث خطأ'; var c = e.code || '';
      if (e.message === 'timeout') msg = 'انتهت المهلة — حاول مجدداً';
      else if (c === 'auth/invalid-email') msg = 'البريد غير صحيح';
      else if (c === 'auth/user-not-found') msg = 'لا يوجد حساب بهذا البريد';
      else if (c === 'auth/wrong-password') msg = 'كلمة المرور غير صحيحة';
      else if (c === 'auth/invalid-credential') msg = 'البريد أو كلمة المرور غير صحيحة';
      else if (c === 'auth/too-many-requests') msg = 'محاولات كثيرة — حاول لاحقاً';
      else if (c === 'auth/network-request-failed') msg = 'تحقق من الإنترنت';
      this.showErr('login-err', msg);
    }
  },
  signup: async function() {
    if (this._busy) return;
    var name = (document.getElementById('signup-name').value || '').trim(); var email = (document.getElementById('signup-email').value || '').trim().toLowerCase(); var phone = (document.getElementById('signup-phone').value || '').trim(); var pass = document.getElementById('signup-password').value || ''; var confirm = document.getElementById('signup-confirm').value || ''; var terms = document.getElementById('signup-terms').checked;
    if (name.length < 2) { this.showErr('signup-err', 'أدخل اسمك الكامل'); return; }
    if (!this.isEmail(email)) { this.showErr('signup-err', 'أدخل بريداً صحيحاً'); return; }
    if (!this.isPhone(phone)) { this.showErr('signup-err', 'أدخل رقم جوال صحيح'); return; }
    if (pass.length < 6) { this.showErr('signup-err', 'كلمة المرور 6 أحرف على الأقل'); return; }
    if (pass !== confirm) { this.showErr('signup-err', 'كلمتا المرور غير متطابقتين'); return; }
    if (!terms) { this.showErr('signup-err', 'يجب الموافقة على الشروط والأحكام'); return; }
    if (!this._fbReady) { this.showErr('signup-err', 'جاري التحميل — حاول بعد لحظات'); return; }
    this._setBusy(true); this.setLoading('btn-signup', true);
    try {
      await withTimeout(FB.signupEmail(email, pass), 20000, null);
      await withTimeout(FB.saveUserProfile({ name: name, email: email, phone: phone, loyaltyPoints: 240, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }), 10000, null);
      try { await withTimeout(FB.updateDisplayName(name), 5000, null); } catch(e) {}
      this.redirect();
    } catch (e) {
      this.setLoading('btn-signup', false); this._setBusy(false);
      var msg = 'حدث خطأ'; var c = e.code || '';
      if (e.message === 'timeout') msg = 'انتهت المهلة — حاول مجدداً';
      else if (c === 'auth/email-already-in-use') msg = 'البريد مسجل بالفعل';
      else if (c === 'auth/weak-password') msg = 'كلمة المرور ضعيفة';
      else if (c === 'auth/invalid-email') msg = 'البريد غير صحيح';
      else if (c === 'auth/network-request-failed') msg = 'تحقق من الإنترنت';
      this.showErr('signup-err', msg);
    }
  },
  googleLogin: async function() {
    if (this._busy) return;
    /* تم إزالة فحص _fbReady — Firebase جاهز دائماً */
    this._setBusy(true); this.setLoading('btn-google', true);
    try {
      var provider = new firebase.auth.GoogleAuthProvider(); provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
      var isMobile = /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
      if (isMobile) { this.showOverlay('جاري التحويل إلى Google...'); await FB.auth.signInWithRedirect(provider); return; }
      var result = await withTimeout(FB.auth.signInWithPopup(provider), 60000, null); if (!result) throw new Error('timeout');
      if (result.user) {
        var existing = await withTimeout(FB.getUserProfile(), 5000, null);
        if (!existing) { await withTimeout(FB.saveUserProfile({ name: result.user.displayName || 'مستخدم', email: result.user.email, phone: '', loyaltyPoints: 240, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }), 8000, null); }
        this.redirect();
      } else { this.setLoading('btn-google', false); this._setBusy(false); }
    } catch (e) {
      this.setLoading('btn-google', false); this._setBusy(false); this.hideOverlay();
      var c = e.code || ''; if (c === 'auth/popup-closed-by-user' || c === 'auth/cancelled-popup-request') return;
      if (e.message === 'timeout') { this.showErr('login-err', 'انتهت المهلة'); return; }
      var msg = 'فشل الدخول بـ Google';
      if (c === 'auth/unauthorized-domain') msg = 'النطاق غير مصرح';
      else if (c === 'auth/network-request-failed') msg = 'تحقق من الإنترنت';
      this.showErr('login-err', msg);
    }
  },
  facebookLogin: async function() {
    if (this._busy) return;
    /* تم إزالة فحص _fbReady — Firebase جاهز دائماً */
    this._setBusy(true); this.setLoading('btn-facebook', true);
    try {
      var provider = new firebase.auth.FacebookAuthProvider(); provider.setCustomParameters({ display: 'popup' });
      var isMobile = /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
      if (isMobile) { this.showOverlay('جاري التحويل إلى Facebook...'); await FB.auth.signInWithRedirect(provider); return; }
      var result = await withTimeout(FB.auth.signInWithPopup(provider), 60000, null); if (!result) throw new Error('timeout');
      if (result.user) {
        var existing = await withTimeout(FB.getUserProfile(), 5000, null);
        if (!existing) { await withTimeout(FB.saveUserProfile({ name: result.user.displayName || 'مستخدم', email: result.user.email || '', phone: '', loyaltyPoints: 240, createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }), 8000, null); }
        this.redirect();
      } else { this.setLoading('btn-facebook', false); this._setBusy(false); }
    } catch (e) {
      this.setLoading('btn-facebook', false); this._setBusy(false); this.hideOverlay();
      var c = e.code || ''; if (c === 'auth/popup-closed-by-user' || c === 'auth/cancelled-popup-request') return;
      if (e.message === 'timeout') { this.showErr('login-err', 'انتهت المهلة'); return; }
      var msg = 'فشل الدخول بـ Facebook';
      if (c === 'auth/unauthorized-domain') msg = 'النطاق غير مصرح';
      else if (c === 'auth/account-exists-with-different-credential') msg = 'هذا البريد مسجل بطريقة أخرى';
      else if (c === 'auth/network-request-failed') msg = 'تحقق من الإنترنت';
      this.showErr('login-err', msg);
    }
  },
  sendReset: async function() {
    if (this._busy) return;
    var email = (document.getElementById('forgot-email').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!this.isEmail(email)) { this.showErr('forgot-err', 'أدخل بريداً صحيحاً'); return; }
    if (!this._fbReady) { this.showErr('forgot-err', 'جاري التحميل — حاول بعد لحظات'); return; }
    this._setBusy(true); this.setLoading('btn-forgot', true);
    try {
      await withTimeout(FB.sendPasswordReset(email), 15000, null);
      this.setLoading('btn-forgot', false); this._setBusy(false); this.toast('تم إرسال الرابط ✓', 'success');
      var self = this; setTimeout(function(){ self.showScreen('login'); }, 1500);
    } catch (e) {
      this.setLoading('btn-forgot', false); this._setBusy(false);
      var msg = 'حدث خطأ'; if (e.message === 'timeout') msg = 'انتهت المهلة';
      else if (e.code === 'auth/user-not-found') msg = 'لا يوجد حساب بهذا البريد';
      else if (e.code === 'auth/invalid-email') msg = 'البريد غير صحيح';
      this.showErr('forgot-err', msg);
    }
  },
  toast: function(msg, type) { var t = document.getElementById('toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.className = 'toast ' + (type || ''); t.classList.add('show'); clearTimeout(window._toastTimer); window._toastTimer = setTimeout(function(){ t.classList.remove('show'); }, 2800); }
};
function togglePassword(id, btn) { var inp = document.getElementById(id); if (!inp) return; if (inp.type === 'password') { inp.type = 'text'; btn.classList.add('active'); } else { inp.type = 'password'; btn.classList.remove('active'); } }
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) { var dd = document.getElementById('country-dropdown'); if (dd && !e.target.closest('.country-select')) dd.classList.remove('show'); });
if (document.readyState === 'loading') { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){ AUTH.init(); }); } else { AUTH.init(); }
document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
  if (e.key !== 'Enter') return; if (AUTH._busy) return; var active = document.querySelector('.screen.active'); if (!active) return;
  if (active.id === 'screen-login')  { e.preventDefault(); AUTH.login(); }
  if (active.id === 'screen-signup') { e.preventDefault(); AUTH.signup(); }
  if (active.id === 'screen-forgot') { e.preventDefault(); AUTH.sendReset(); }
  if (active.id === 'screen-phone')  { e.preventDefault(); AUTH.sendPhoneOTP(); }
});
if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) { window.addEventListener('load', function() { navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(function(){}); }); }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
function goBack() {
  if (window.history.length > 1) window.history.back();
  else window.location.href = 'chizen.html';
}

(function() {
  var c = {phone:'+967 77 123 4567', whatsapp:'967771234567', email:'support@chizen.com'};
  try {
    var s = localStorage.getItem('chizen_contact');
    if (s) c = Object.assign(c, JSON.parse(s));
  } catch(e) {}

  var el = document.getElementById('contact-info');
  if (el) {
    el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="contact-row"><div class="contact-icon">📱</div><div class="contact-text"><div class="contact-label">الهاتف</div><div class="contact-value">' + c.phone + '</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="contact-row"><div class="contact-icon">💬</div><div class="contact-text"><div class="contact-label">واتساب</div><div class="contact-value">+' + c.whatsapp + '</div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="contact-row"><div class="contact-icon">✉️</div><div class="contact-text"><div class="contact-label">البريد الإلكتروني</div><div class="contact-value">' + c.email + '</div></div></div>';
  }

  try {
    var st = localStorage.getItem('chizen_state');
    if (st) {
      var p = JSON.parse(st);
      if (p.theme === 'light') document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
      document.documentElement.lang = p.lang || 'ar';
      document.documentElement.dir = (p.lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr');
    }
  } catch(e) {}
})();
</script>
</body>

<script src="https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-app-compat.js"></script>
<script src="https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth-compat.js"></script>
<script src="https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore-compat.js"></script>
