<script>
var state = {lang:'ar', theme:'dark', notifications:true, offers:true, orderNotifs:true, biometric:false, fontSize:'medium', user:null, loyaltyPoints:0};
var _pendingBiometric = false;

/* ═══ Dialog helpers ═══ */
function showPrompt(title,defVal,cb,opts){
  opts=opts||{};
  var box=document.getElementById('dialog-box');
  var html='<div class="dialog-title">'+esc(title)+'</div>';
  html+='<input type="'+(opts.type||'text')+'" id="dialog-input" class="dialog-input" value="'+esc(defVal||'')+'" placeholder="'+esc(opts.placeholder||'')+'">';
  html+='<div class="dialog-actions"><button class="dialog-btn cancel" onclick="closeDialog()">إلغاء</button><button class="dialog-btn primary" onclick="submitPrompt()">موافق</button></div>';
  box.innerHTML=html;
  window._promptCb=cb;
  document.getElementById('dialog-overlay').classList.add('active');
  setTimeout(function(){var i=document.getElementById('dialog-input');if(i){i.focus();i.select();i.onkeydown=function(e){if(e.key==='Enter')submitPrompt();};}},100);
}
function submitPrompt(){var i=document.getElementById('dialog-input');var v=i?i.value:'';closeDialog();if(window._promptCb)window._promptCb(v);}
function showConfirm(title,msg,cb){
  var box=document.getElementById('dialog-box');
  var html='<div class="dialog-title">'+esc(title)+'</div>';
  if(msg)html+='<div class="dialog-message">'+esc(msg)+'</div>';
  html+='<div class="dialog-actions"><button class="dialog-btn cancel" onclick="closeDialog()">إلغاء</button><button class="dialog-btn danger" onclick="submitConfirm()">تأكيد</button></div>';
  box.innerHTML=html;
  window._confirmCb=cb;
  document.getElementById('dialog-overlay').classList.add('active');
}
function submitConfirm(){closeDialog();if(window._confirmCb)window._confirmCb(true);}
function closeDialog(){document.getElementById('dialog-overlay').classList.remove('active');}

/* ═══ INIT ═══ */
function init(){
  loadState();
  applyState();
  renderUser();
  checkCacheSize();
  checkBiometricSupport();

  setTimeout(function() {
    if (window.FB && FB.ready) {
      var user = FB.currentUser;
      if (user) {
        state.user = {
          name: user.displayName || (user.email || '').split('@')[0],
          email: user.email,
          phone: state.user ? state.user.phone : '',
          uid: user.uid
        };
        FB.getUserProfile().then(function(profile) {
          if (profile) {
            if (profile.name) state.user.name = profile.name;
            if (profile.phone) state.user.phone = profile.phone;
            if (typeof profile.loyaltyPoints === 'number') state.loyaltyPoints = profile.loyaltyPoints;
            renderUser();
          }
        }).catch(function(){});
      } else {
        FB.auth.onAuthStateChanged(function(u) {
          if (!u) { window.location.href = 'index.html'; return; }
          state.user = {name: u.displayName || (u.email || '').split('@')[0], email: u.email, phone: '', uid: u.uid};
          renderUser();
        });
      }
    } else {
      if (!state.user) { window.location.href = 'index.html'; return; }
    }
  }, 100);
}

function loadState(){
  try{
    var s = localStorage.getItem('chizen_state');
    if(s){
      var p = JSON.parse(s);
      for(var k in p) if(p[k] !== undefined && p[k] !== null) state[k] = p[k];
    }
  }catch(e){}
}

function saveState(){
  try{localStorage.setItem('chizen_state', JSON.stringify(state));}catch(e){}
  if (window.FB && FB.ready && FB.currentUser) {
    FB.saveUserProfile({
      lang: state.lang,
      theme: state.theme,
      fontSize: state.fontSize,
      notifications: state.notifications,
      biometric: state.biometric,
      loyaltyPoints: state.loyaltyPoints
    }).catch(function(){});
  }
}

function applyState(){
  document.documentElement.lang = state.lang;
  document.documentElement.dir = state.lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
  if (state.theme === 'light') document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
  else document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');

  var langEl = document.getElementById('v-lang'); if (langEl) langEl.textContent = state.lang === 'ar' ? 'العربية' : 'English';
  var themeEl = document.getElementById('v-theme'); if (themeEl) themeEl.textContent = state.theme === 'dark' ? 'داكن' : 'فاتح';
  var fontEl = document.getElementById('v-font'); if (fontEl) fontEl.textContent = state.fontSize === 'small' ? 'صغير' : state.fontSize === 'large' ? 'كبير' : 'متوسط';

  setToggle('t-notif', state.notifications);
  setToggle('t-offers', state.offers);
  setToggle('t-orders', state.orderNotifs);
  setToggle('t-bio', state.biometric);

  document.body.style.fontSize = state.fontSize === 'small' ? '14px' : state.fontSize === 'large' ? '18px' : '16px';
}

function setToggle(id, v){
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!el) return;
  if (v) el.classList.add('on');
  else el.classList.remove('on');
}

function renderUser(){
  var u = state.user || {name:'User', email:''};
  var av = document.getElementById('mini-avatar'); if (av) av.textContent = (u.name || 'U').charAt(0);
  var nm = document.getElementById('mini-name'); if (nm) nm.textContent = u.name || 'User';
  var em = document.getElementById('mini-email'); if (em) em.textContent = u.email || '';
}

/* ═══ BIOMETRIC ═══ */
function checkBiometricSupport(){
  try {
    if (window.PublicKeyCredential && PublicKeyCredential.isUserVerifyingPlatformAuthenticatorAvailable) {
      PublicKeyCredential.isUserVerifyingPlatformAuthenticatorAvailable().then(function(ok) {
        var desc = document.getElementById('bio-desc');
        if (!ok && desc) desc.textContent = 'البصمة غير متوفرة على هذا الجهاز';
      });
    } else {
      var desc = document.getElementById('bio-desc');
      if (desc) desc.textContent = 'المتصفح لا يدعم البصمة';
    }
  } catch(e) {}
}

async function isBiometricAvailable(){
  try {
    if (!window.PublicKeyCredential || !PublicKeyCredential.isUserVerifyingPlatformAuthenticatorAvailable) return false;
    return await PublicKeyCredential.isUserVerifyingPlatformAuthenticatorAvailable();
  } catch(e) { return false; }
}

async function registerBiometric(){
  try {
    var uid = (state.user && state.user.uid) || (state.user && state.user.email) || 'user';
    var challenge = new Uint8Array(32);
    crypto.getRandomValues(challenge);
    var userId = new TextEncoder().encode(uid);

    var cred = await navigator.credentials.create({
      publicKey: {
        challenge: challenge,
        rp: { name: 'CHIZEN', id: window.location.hostname || 'localhost' },
        user: {
          id: userId,
          name: (state.user && state.user.email) || 'user@chizen.app',
          displayName: (state.user && state.user.name) || 'User'
        },
        pubKeyCredParams: [
          { type: 'public-key', alg: -7 },
          { type: 'public-key', alg: -257 }
        ],
        authenticatorSelection: {
          authenticatorAttachment: 'platform',
          userVerification: 'required',
          requireResidentKey: false
        },
        timeout: 60000,
        attestation: 'none'
      }
    });

    if (cred && cred.id) {
      localStorage.setItem('chizen_bio_cred_id', cred.id);
      state.biometric = true;
      saveState();
      applyState();
      toast('تم تفعيل البصمة ✓');
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  } catch(e) {
    console.log('Bio register error:', e);
    if (e.name === 'NotAllowedError') toast('تم إلغاء البصمة');
    else if (e.name === 'NotSupportedError') toast('البصمة غير مدعومة');
    else toast('فشل تفعيل البصمة');
    return false;
  }
}

async function verifyBiometric(){
  try {
    var credId = localStorage.getItem('chizen_bio_cred_id');
    if (!credId) return false;

    var credIdBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(credId.replace(/-/g,'+').replace(/_/g,'/')), function(c) { return c.charCodeAt(0); });
    var challenge = new Uint8Array(32);
    crypto.getRandomValues(challenge);

    var assertion = await navigator.credentials.get({
      publicKey: {
        challenge: challenge,
        rpId: window.location.hostname || 'localhost',
        allowCredentials: [{ type: 'public-key', id: credIdBytes }],
        userVerification: 'required',
        timeout: 60000
      }
    });
    return !!(assertion && assertion.id);
  } catch(e) {
    console.log('Bio verify error:', e);
    return false;
  }
}

async function toggleBiometric(){
  if (state.biometric) {
    showConfirm('إيقاف البصمة', 'هل تريد إيقاف القفل بالبصمة؟', function(ok) {
      if (!ok) return;
      state.biometric = false;
      localStorage.removeItem('chizen_bio_cred_id');
      saveState();
      applyState();
      toast('تم إيقاف البصمة');
    });
  } else {
    var available = await isBiometricAvailable();
    if (!available) {
      toast('البصمة غير متوفرة على هذا الجهاز');
      return;
    }
    await registerBiometric();
  }
}

function doBiometric(){
  verifyBiometric().then(function(ok) {
    if (ok) {
      document.getElementById('biometric-overlay').classList.remove('active');
      var text = document.getElementById('bio-text');
      var sub = document.getElementById('bio-sub');
      var btn = document.getElementById('bio-btn');
      if (text) text.textContent = 'تم التحقق ✓';
      if (sub) sub.textContent = 'مرحباً بك';
      if (btn) btn.style.display = 'none';
      setTimeout(function() {
        document.getElementById('biometric-overlay').classList.remove('active');
        _pendingBiometric = false;
      }, 500);
    } else {
      toast('فشل التحقق');
    }
  });
}

/* ═══ MODALS ═══ */
function openLanguage(){
  openModal('اختر اللغة', [{v:'ar',l:'العربية'}, {v:'en',l:'English'}], state.lang, function(v){
    state.lang = v; applyState(); saveState(); toast('تم تطبيق اللغة ✓');
  });
}
function openTheme(){
  openModal('اختر الوضع', [{v:'dark',l:'داكن'}, {v:'light',l:'فاتح'}], state.theme, function(v){
    state.theme = v; applyState(); saveState(); toast('تم تطبيق الوضع ✓');
  });
}
function openFont(){
  openModal('حجم الخط', [{v:'small',l:'صغير'}, {v:'medium',l:'متوسط'}, {v:'large',l:'كبير'}], state.fontSize, function(v){
    state.fontSize = v; applyState(); saveState(); toast('تم التطبيق ✓');
  });
}
function openModal(title, opts, current, cb){
  document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent = title;
  var html = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < opts.length; i++) {
    var o = opts[i];
    var sel = o.v === current ? ' selected' : '';
    html += '<div class="modal-option' + sel + '" onclick="selectModal(\'' + o.v + '\')"><span>' + o.l + '</span></div>';
  }
  document.getElementById('modal-content').innerHTML = html;
  window._modalCb = cb;
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.add('active');
}
function selectModal(v){ if (window._modalCb) window._modalCb(v); closeModal(); }
function closeModal(){ document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('active'); }

function toggleT(k, el){
  state[k] = !state[k];
  if (state[k]) el.classList.add('on'); else el.classList.remove('on');
  if (k === 'notifications' && state[k] && 'Notification' in window && Notification.permission === 'default') {
    Notification.requestPermission();
  }
  saveState();
  toast('تم الحفظ ✓');
}

function checkCacheSize(){
  var size = 0;
  try { for (var k in localStorage) if (localStorage.hasOwnProperty(k)) size += localStorage[k].length; } catch(e) {}
  var el = document.getElementById('cache-size');
  if (el) el.textContent = Math.round(size / 1024) + ' KB مستخدمة';
}

async function clearCache(){
  showConfirm('مسح الذاكرة المؤقتة', 'سيتم مسح الملفات المؤقتة، لكن بياناتك ستبقى.', async function(ok){
    if (!ok) return;
    try {
      if ('caches' in window) {
        var keys = await caches.keys();
        for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) await caches.delete(keys[i]);
      }
      var keep = ['chizen_state','chizen_users','chizen_cart','chizen_orders','chizen_favorites','chizen_addresses','chizen_notifications','chizen_deals','chizen_contact','chizen_ads','chizen_categories','chizen_payments','chizen_bio_cred_id'];
      for (var k in localStorage) if (localStorage.hasOwnProperty(k) && keep.indexOf(k) === -1) localStorage.removeItem(k);
    } catch(e) {}
    toast('تم ✓');
    checkCacheSize();
  });
}

function backupData(){
  var data = {};
  try {
    var keys = ['chizen_state','chizen_users','chizen_cart','chizen_orders','chizen_favorites','chizen_addresses','chizen_notifications','chizen_contact','chizen_categories','chizen_payments'];
    for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { var v = localStorage.getItem(keys[i]); if (v) data[keys[i]] = v; }
  } catch(e) {}
  var blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], {type:'application/json'});
  var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  var a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url; a.download = 'chizen-backup-' + Date.now() + '.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  toast('تم ✓');
}

function editProfile(){
  var u = state.user || {name:'User', email:''};
  showPrompt('الاسم الكامل', u.name || '', function(name){
    if (name === null) return;
    if (name.length < 2) { toast('الاسم قصير'); return; }
    state.user.name = name;
    saveState();
    renderUser();
    if (window.FB && FB.ready) {
      try { FB.updateDisplayName(name); FB.saveUserProfile({name:name}); } catch(e){}
    }
    toast('تم ✓');
  });
}

function changePassword(){
  showPrompt('كلمة المرور الحالية', '', function(oldP){
    if (!oldP) return;
    if (window.FB && FB.ready && FB.currentUser) {
      try {
        var cred = firebase.auth.EmailAuthProvider.credential(FB.currentUser.email, oldP);
        FB.currentUser.reauthenticateWithCredential(cred).then(function(){
          showPrompt('كلمة المرور الجديدة (6+)', '', function(np){
            if (!np || np.length < 6) { toast('كلمة المرور قصيرة'); return; }
            showPrompt('تأكيد كلمة المرور', '', function(cp){
              if (np !== cp) { toast('غير متطابقة'); return; }
              FB.currentUser.updatePassword(np).then(function(){ toast('تم ✓'); }).catch(function(){ toast('فشل'); });
            }, {type:'password'});
          }, {type:'password'});
        }).catch(function(){ toast('كلمة المرور غير صحيحة'); });
      } catch(e) { toast('فشل'); }
    } else {
      toast('غير مدعوم');
    }
  }, {type:'password'});
}

function deleteAccount(){
  showConfirm('حذف الحساب نهائياً', '⚠️ لا يمكن الرجوع.', function(ok){
    if (!ok) return;
    showConfirm('تأكيد أخير', 'هل أنت متأكد؟', async function(ok2){
      if (!ok2) return;
      try {
        if (window.FB && FB.ready && FB.currentUser) {
          try { await FB.db.collection('users').doc(FB.currentUser.uid).delete(); } catch(e){}
          try { await FB.currentUser.delete(); } catch(e){}
        }
        var keys = ['chizen_state','chizen_cart','chizen_orders','chizen_favorites','chizen_addresses','chizen_notifications','chizen_bio_cred_id'];
        for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) localStorage.removeItem(keys[i]);
      } catch(e){}
      window.location.href = 'index.html';
    });
  });
}

function rateApp(){
  showPrompt('قيّم التطبيق من 1 إلى 5', '', function(rating){
    if (!rating) return;
    rating = parseInt(rating);
    if (rating < 1 || rating > 5) { toast('اختر من 1 إلى 5'); return; }
    try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_rating', rating); } catch(e){}
    toast('شكراً لتقييمك! ⭐');
  });
}

function shareApp(){
  var text = 'جرب تطبيق CHIZEN!';
  if (navigator.share) navigator.share({title:'CHIZEN', text:text}).catch(function(){});
  else toast('المشاركة غير مدعومة');
}

function logout(){
  showConfirm('تسجيل الخروج', 'هل أنت متأكد؟', async function(ok){
    if (!ok) return;
    try { if (window.FB && FB.ready) await FB.logout(); } catch(e){}
    try { localStorage.removeItem('chizen_state'); } catch(e){}
    window.location.href = 'index.html';
  });
}

function toast(msg){
  var t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(window._tt);
  window._tt = setTimeout(function(){ t.classList.remove('show'); }, 2500);
}

if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(function(){});
  });
}

if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
else init();

  <div class="content">
    <div class="hero">
      <div class="hero-icon">📋</div>
      <div class="hero-title">الشروط والأحكام</div>
      <div class="hero-date">آخر تحديث: يناير 2025</div>
    </div>

    <div class="highlight">
      <div class="highlight-title">⚠️ مهم</div>
      <p>باستخدامك تطبيق CHIZEN، فأنت توافق على الشروط أدناه. إذا لم توافق، نرجو عدم استخدام التطبيق.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">1</span> قبول الشروط</div>
      <p>عند إنشاء حساب أو استخدام التطبيق، فإنك تقر بأنك قرأت هذه الشروط وفهمتها ووافقت عليها. إذا كنت دون 18 عاماً، يجب أن يكون استخدامك بإشراف والد أو ولي أمر.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">2</span> حسابك</div>
      <p>أنت مسؤول عن:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>صحة المعلومات التي تسجّلها (الاسم، البريد، الجوال)</li>
        <li>الحفاظ على سرية كلمة المرور</li>
        <li>كل نشاط يحدث من خلال حسابك</li>
      </ul>
      <p>إذا لاحظت أي استخدام غير مصرح به لحسابك، تواصل معنا فوراً.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">3</span> الطلبات</div>
      <p>عند تأكيد أي طلب، يمر بالمراحل التالية:</p>
      <ul>
        <li><strong>قيد المعالجة</strong> — نتحقق من الحوالة</li>
        <li><strong>في الطريق</strong> — الطلب في مرحلة التوصيل</li>
        <li><strong>مكتمل</strong> — استلمت الطلب</li>
      </ul>
      <p>نحتفظ بالحق في إلغاء أي طلب لأسباب مثل: عدم توفر المنتج، خطأ في السعر، أو شك في صحة الحوالة. عند الإلغاء، يُعاد المبلغ كاملاً.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">4</span> الأسعار والدفع</div>
      <p>جميع الأسعار بالريال السعودي، وتشمل الضريبة إن وُجدت. طريقة الدفع الأساسية:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>حوالة بنكية لأحد البنوك المعتمدة في التطبيق</li>
        <li>الدفع النقدي عند التسليم (في حال توفره)</li>
      </ul>
      <p>لا نقبل بطاقات الائتمان حالياً. لا نحتفظ ببيانات بنكية.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">5</span> الشحن والتسليم</div>
      <p>نوصّل إلى نقاط تسليم محددة في كل مدينة. المدة:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>داخل المدينة: 1-2 أيام عمل</li>
        <li>مدن أخرى: 2-5 أيام عمل</li>
      </ul>
      <p>التأخير لأسباب خارجة عن إرادتنا (طقس، إغلاق طرق) لا يُلزمنا بتعويض.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">6</span> الإرجاع والاستبدال</div>
      <p>يمكنك إرجاع المنتج خلال <strong>7 أيام</strong> من الاستلام إذا:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>المنتج بحالته الأصلية</li>
        <li>العلبة والتغليف سليمين</li>
        <li>لم يُستخدم المنتج</li>
      </ul>
      <p><strong>لا يُقبل الإرجاع</strong> في: المنتجات المفتوحة لأسباب صحية (عطور، مستحضرات تجميل، ملابس داخلية)، والمنتجات المُخصصة حسب الطلب.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">7</span> نقاط الولاء</div>
      <p>تحصل على <strong>نقطة واحدة لكل 10 ر.س</strong> تنفقها. النقاط:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>لا تُصرف نقداً</li>
        <li>لا تُنقل بين الحسابات</li>
        <li>تنتهي إذا حُذف الحساب</li>
      </ul>
      <p>نحتفظ بالحق في تعديل نظام النقاط مع إشعار مسبق.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">8</span> السلوك المحظور</div>
      <p>يُمنع استخدام التطبيق في:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>أي نشاط غير قانوني</li>
        <li>محاولات اختراق أو تعديل التطبيق</li>
        <li>إساءة استخدام نظام النقاط</li>
        <li>انتحال شخصية أو تقديم معلومات كاذبة</li>
      </ul>
      <p>في حال المخالفة، قد نُعلّق حسابك أو نحذفه نهائياً.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">9</span> حد مسؤوليتنا</div>
      <p>نقدّم التطبيق "كما هو". لا نتحمل مسؤولية:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>انقطاع الخدمة لأسباب تقنية خارجة عن إرادتنا</li>
        <li>أي خسارة ناتجة عن سوء استخدامك للحساب</li>
        <li>جودة المنتجات من أطراف خارجية</li>
      </ul>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">10</span> التعديلات على الشروط</div>
      <p>قد نُحدّث هذه الشروط من وقت لآخر. التحديثات تسري فور نشرها في التطبيق. استمرارك في الاستخدام يعني موافقتك.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="section">
      <div class="section-title"><span class="section-num">11</span> القانون المطبق</div>
      <p>تخضع هذه الشروط للقوانين المعمول بها في بلد إقامة مالك التطبيق. أي نزاع يُحلّ ودياً، وإلا فيُحال للجهات المختصة.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="footer">
      <div class="footer-logo">CHIZEN</div>

function goBack() {
  if (window.history.length > 1) window.history.back();
  else window.location.href = 'chizen.html';
}

(function() {
  try {
    var st = localStorage.getItem('chizen_state');
    if (st) {
      var p = JSON.parse(st);
      if (p.theme === 'light') document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
      document.documentElement.lang = p.lang || 'ar';
      document.documentElement.dir = (p.lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr');
    }
  } catch(e) {}
})();
</script>

  <div id="s-invalid" class="state invalid"><div class="icon">✗</div><div class="title" style="color:var(--red);">سند غير صالح</div><div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;">لم يتم العثور على هذا الطلب في النظام</div></div>

<script src="https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-app-compat.js"></script>

<script>
(async function(){
  var id = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('id');
  if(!id){ document.getElementById('s-loading').classList.remove('active'); document.getElementById('s-invalid').classList.add('active'); return; }
  try {
    if(!firebase.apps.length) firebase.initializeApp(window.FIREBASE_CONFIG);
    var doc = await firebase.firestore().collection('verifications').doc(String(id).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_')).get();
    document.getElementById('s-loading').classList.remove('active');
    if(doc.exists){
      var d = doc.data();
      var h = '<div class="row"><span class="label">رقم الطلب</span><span class="val" style="color:var(--pink);">#'+d.orderId+'</span></div>';
      h += '<div class="row"><span class="label">العميل</span><span class="val">'+d.customerName+'</span></div>';
      h += '<div class="row"><span class="label">الهاتف</span><span class="val" style="direction:ltr;">'+d.customerPhone+'</span></div>';
      h += '<div class="row"><span class="label">الإجمالي</span><span class="val" style="color:var(--pink);">'+d.total+' ر.س</span></div>';
      document.getElementById('v-details').innerHTML = h;
      document.getElementById('s-valid').classList.add('active');
    } else {
      document.getElementById('s-invalid').classList.add('active');
    }
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById('s-loading').classList.remove('active');
    document.getElementById('s-invalid').classList.add('active');
  }
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

===== FILE: bridge.js =====
/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════
   CHIZEN BRIDGE v2
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */

window.Bridge = {

  getProducts: function() {
    var fbProducts = [];
    try { fbProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_fb_products') || '[]'); } catch(e) {}

    var adminProducts = [];
    try { adminProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_admin_products') || '[]'); } catch(e) {}

    var merged = (window.DEFAULT_PRODUCTS || [])
      .concat(adminProducts)
      .concat(fbProducts);

    var seen = {};
    var out = [];
    for (var i = merged.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var p = merged[i];
      if (p && p.id != null && !seen[p.id]) {
        seen[p.id] = true;
        out.unshift(p);
      }
    }
    return out;
  },

  getBanners: function() {
    try {
      var b = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_banners') || 'null');
      if (b && b.length > 0) return b;
    } catch(e) {}
    return window.DEFAULT_BANNERS || [];
  },

  getCategories: function() {
    try {
      var c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_categories') || 'null');
      if (c && c.length > 0) return c;
    } catch(e) {}
    return window.DEFAULT_CATEGORIES || [];
  },

  getPayments: function() {
    try {
      var p = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_payments') || 'null');
      if (p && p.length > 0) return p;
    } catch(e) {}
    return window.DEFAULT_PAYMENTS || [];
  },

  getContact: function() {
    try {
      var c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_contact') || '{}');
      return {
        phone:    c.phone    || '+967 77 123 4567',
        whatsapp: c.whatsapp || '967771234567',
        email:    c.email    || 'support@chizen.com'
      };
    } catch(e) {
      return { phone:'+967 77 123 4567', whatsapp:'967771234567', email:'support@chizen.com' };
    }
  },

  getAds: function() {
    try {
      var a = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_ads') || '{}');
      return {
        adsenseId: a.adsenseId || '',
        admobId:   a.admobId   || '',
        mode:      a.mode      || 'placeholder'
      };
    } catch(e) {
      return { adsenseId:'', admobId:'', mode:'placeholder' };
    }
  },

  getDeals: function() {
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_deals') || 'null'); } catch(e) { return null; }
  },

  getNotifications: function() {
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_notifications') || '[]'); } catch(e) { return []; }
  },

  renderAd: function(slotId) {
    var el = document.getElementById(slotId);
    if (!el) return;
    var ads = this.getAds();
    if (ads.mode === 'off') { el.style.display = 'none'; return; }

    if (ads.mode === 'adsense' && ads.adsenseId) {
      el.innerHTML = '<ins class="adsbygoogle" style="display:block" data-ad-client="'
        + window.esc(ads.adsenseId) + '" data-ad-format="auto" data-full-width-responsive="true"></ins>';
      try { (window.adsbygoogle = window.adsbygoogle || []).push({}); } catch(e) {}
    } else if (ads.mode === 'admob' && ads.admobId) {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="ad-content">AdMob: ' + window.esc(ads.admobId) + '</div>';
    } else {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="ad-content">مساحة إعلانية</div>';
    }
  },

  cacheImages: async function() {
    if (!('caches' in window)) return;
    try {
      var cache = await caches.open('chizen-images-v1');
      var products = this.getProducts();
      var banners = this.getBanners();
      var urls = [];
      products.forEach(function(p){ if (p.img) urls.push(p.img); });
      banners.forEach(function(b){ if (b.img) urls.push(b.img); });
      for (var i = 0; i < urls.length; i++) {
        var cached = await cache.match(urls[i]);
        if (!cached) { try { await cache.add(urls[i]); } catch(e) {} }
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  },

  syncFromFirebase: async function() {
    if (!window.FB || !FB.ready) return false;
    try {
      var products = await FB.getProducts();
      if (products && products.length) {
        var mapped = products.map(function(p) {
          return {
            id: p.id, category: p.category || 'home', brand: p.brand || '',
            name: p.name || '', nameEn: p.nameEn || '', price: p.price || 0,
            old: p.old || null, badge: p.badge || '', rating: p.rating || 4.5,
            reviews: p.reviews || 0, sold: p.sold || 0, img: p.img || '',
            desc: p.desc || '', features: p.features || []
          };
        });
        localStorage.setItem('chizen_fb_products', JSON.stringify(mapped));
      }

      var banners = await FB.getBanners();
      if (banners && banners.length) {
        localStorage.setItem('chizen_banners', JSON.stringify(banners));
      }

      var cats = await FB.getConfig('categories');
      if (cats && cats.list) localStorage.setItem('chizen_categories', JSON.stringify(cats.list));

      var pays = await FB.getConfig('payments');
      if (pays && pays.list) localStorage.setItem('chizen_payments', JSON.stringify(pays.list));

      var contact = await FB.getConfig('contact');
      if (contact) localStorage.setItem('chizen_contact', JSON.stringify(contact));

      var ads = await FB.getConfig('ads');
      if (ads) localStorage.setItem('chizen_ads', JSON.stringify(ads));

      var deal = await FB.getDeal();
      if (deal) localStorage.setItem('chizen_deals', JSON.stringify(deal));

      return true;
    } catch(e) {
      console.log('[Bridge] Sync failed:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }
};

      slots: { morning: 'Morning (9-12)', noon: 'Noon (12-3)', afternoon: 'Afternoon (3-6)', evening: 'Evening (6-9)' },
      days: { today: 'Today', tomorrow: 'Tomorrow', after: 'Day After' },
      confirm: 'Confirm', loading: 'Loading...',
      couponIssued: 'Your welcome coupon', couponHint: '15% off — valid 30 days',
      errorNoOrder: 'No orders',
      errorGeneric: 'Select order, day and time',
      copied: 'Copied ✓',
      aiBusy: 'Did not understand. Try: "Track order" or WhatsApp 📱',
      slotBooked: '✅ Delivery slot saved',
      slotFail: 'Booking failed — try later',
      alreadyCoupon: 'You already have a coupon 🎁',
      couponFail: 'Coupon failed',
      loginFirst: 'Please login first'
    }

  const SUGGESTIONS = {
    ar: ['كيف أتتبع طلبي؟', 'متى يوصل الطلب؟', 'طرق الدفع؟', 'شحن مجاني؟'],
    en: ['Track my order?', 'Delivery time?', 'Payment?', 'Free shipping?']
  };

  const state = {
    chatModal: null, chatMessages: null, chatInput: null,
    isOpen: false, isTyping: false, conversationStarted: false,
    currentLang: 'ar', _slotSelected: null, _daySelected: null
  };

  try { state.currentLang = localStorage.getItem('chizen_chat_lang') || 'ar'; } catch(e) {}
  function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])); }
  function t() { return UI_TEXT[state.currentLang]; }
  function nowTime() { return new Date().toLocaleTimeString(state.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'ar-EG' : 'en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }); }

  function createWidget() {
    if (document.getElementById('chizen-chat-root')) return;

    const fab = document.createElement('button');
    fab.className = 'chat-fab';
    fab.setAttribute('aria-label', 'CHIZEN Assistant');
    fab.textContent = '💬';
    fab.onclick = openChat;

    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'chat-overlay';
    overlay.id = 'chat-overlay';
    overlay.innerHTML = `
      <div class="chat-sheet">
        <div class="chat-header">
          <div class="chat-avatar">🤖</div>
          <div class="chat-header-info">
            <div class="chat-header-name"><span id="chat-title">${t().title}</span><span class="chat-status-dot"></span></div>
            <div class="chat-header-sub" id="chat-subtitle">${t().subtitle}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="chat-header-actions">
            <button class="chat-lang-btn" id="chat-lang-btn" onclick="ChizenChat.toggleLang()">${state.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'EN' : 'ع'}</button>
            <button class="chat-wa-btn" onclick="ChizenChat.openWhatsApp()" title="WhatsApp">💬</button>
            <button class="chat-close" onclick="ChizenChat.close()">×</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="chat-messages" id="chat-messages"></div>
        <div class="chat-suggestions" id="chat-suggestions"></div>
        <div class="chat-input-area">
          <textarea class="chat-input" id="chat-input" placeholder="${t().inputPlaceholder}" rows="1"></textarea>
          <button class="chat-send" onclick="ChizenChat.sendMessage()">➤</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    overlay.onclick = e => { if (e.target === overlay) closeChat(); };

    const slotModal = document.createElement('div');
    slotModal.className = 'chat-slot-modal';
    slotModal.id = 'chat-slot-modal';
    slotModal.innerHTML = '<div class="chat-slot-box" id="chat-slot-box"></div>';
    slotModal.onclick = e => { if (e.target === slotModal) closeSlotModal(); };

    const root = document.createElement('div');
    root.id = 'chizen-chat-root';
    root.appendChild(fab);
    root.appendChild(overlay);
    root.appendChild(slotModal);
    document.body.appendChild(root);

    state.chatModal = overlay;
    state.chatMessages = document.getElementById('chat-messages');
    state.chatInput = document.getElementById('chat-input');

    state.chatInput.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); sendMessage(); }
    });
    state.chatInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
      state.chatInput.style.height = 'auto';
      state.chatInput.style.height = Math.min(state.chatInput.scrollHeight, 90) + 'px';
    });
  }

  function openChat() {
    if (!document.getElementById('chizen-chat-root')) createWidget();
    state.chatModal.classList.add('active');
    state.isOpen = true;
    if (!state.conversationStarted) { showWelcome(); state.conversationStarted = true; }
    setTimeout(() => state.chatInput.focus(), 350);
  }
  function closeChat() { state.chatModal.classList.remove('active'); state.isOpen = false; }

  function toggleLang() {
    state.currentLang = state.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar';
    try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_chat_lang', state.currentLang); } catch(e) {}
    document.documentElement.lang = state.currentLang;
    document.documentElement.dir = state.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
    document.getElementById('chat-title').textContent = t().title;
    document.getElementById('chat-subtitle').textContent = t().subtitle;
    document.getElementById('chat-input').placeholder = t().inputPlaceholder;
    document.getElementById('chat-lang-btn').textContent = state.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'EN' : 'ع';
    state.chatMessages.innerHTML = '';
    showWelcome();
  }

  function showWelcome() {
    state.chatMessages.innerHTML = `
      <div class="chat-welcome">
        <div class="chat-welcome-title">${t().welcomeTitle}</div>
        <div>${t().welcomeSub}</div>
        <div class="chat-welcome-options">
          <div class="chat-welcome-option" onclick="ChizenChat.startAI()"><span class="ico">🤖</span><span class="lbl">${t().optAI}</span></div>
          <div class="chat-welcome-option" onclick="ChizenChat.openWhatsApp()"><span class="ico">💬</span><span class="lbl">${t().optSupport}</span></div>
          <div class="chat-welcome-option" onclick="ChizenChat.openSchedule()"><span class="ico">⏱️</span><span class="lbl">${t().optDelivery}</span></div>
          <div class="chat-welcome-option" onclick="ChizenChat.claimCoupon()"><span class="ico">🎁</span><span class="lbl">${t().optCoupon}</span></div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    renderSuggestions(SUGGESTIONS[state.currentLang]);
  }

  function renderSuggestions(list) {
    const c = document.getElementById('chat-suggestions');
    if (!c) return;
    c.innerHTML = list.map(s => `<button class="chat-suggestion" onclick="ChizenChat.askQuick('${s.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">${esc(s)}</button>`).join('');
  }

  function startAI() {
    document.getElementById('chat-suggestions').innerHTML = '';
    addBubble('bot', state.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'تفضل، اكتب سؤالك 👇' : 'Go ahead, type your question 👇');
    state.chatInput.focus();
  }

  async function sendMessage() {
    if (state.isTyping) return;
    const text = state.chatInput.value.trim();
    if (!text) return;

    state.chatInput.value = '';
    state.chatInput.style.height = 'auto';
    addBubble('user', text);
    document.getElementById('chat-suggestions').innerHTML = '';

    // 1. Quick
    const quick = matchQuick(text);
    if (quick) { setTimeout(() => addBubble('bot', quick), 300); return; }

    // 2. FAQ
    const faq = matchFAQ(text);
    if (faq) { setTimeout(() => addBubble('bot', faq.a), 400); return; }

    // 3. Fallback
    setTimeout(() => {
      addBubble('bot', t().aiBusy);
      renderSuggestions(SUGGESTIONS[state.currentLang]);
    }, 500);
  }

  function matchQuick(text) {
    const lower = text.toLowerCase();
    for (const item of QUICK_REPLIES[state.currentLang]) {
      for (const k of item.keys) if (lower.includes(k)) return item.text;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function matchFAQ(text) {
    const lower = text.toLowerCase();
    for (const item of FAQ[state.currentLang]) {
      for (const tag of item.tags) if (lower.includes(tag)) return item;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function addBubble(role, content, extraClass) {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'chat-msg ' + (extraClass || role);
    div.textContent = content;
    const time = document.createElement('span');
    time.className = 'chat-msg-time';
    time.textContent = nowTime();
    div.appendChild(time);
    state.chatMessages.appendChild(div);
    state.chatMessages.scrollTop = state.chatMessages.scrollHeight;
  }

  function showTyping() {
    const t2 = document.createElement('div');
    t2.className = 'chat-typing';
    t2.id = 'chat-typing';
    t2.innerHTML = '<span></span><span></span><span></span>';
    state.chatMessages.appendChild(t2);
    state.chatMessages.scrollTop = state.chatMessages.scrollHeight;
  }
  function hideTyping() { const el = document.getElementById('chat-typing'); if (el) el.remove(); }

  /* ═══ حجز التوصيل — حفظ مباشر بدون Cloud Function ═══ */
  function openSchedule() {
    const box = document.getElementById('chat-slot-box');
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="chat-slot-title">⏱️ ${t().optDelivery}</div>
      <label class="chat-slot-label">${t().selectOrder}</label>
      <select class="chat-slot-opt" id="chat-slot-order" style="width:100%;padding:12px;margin-bottom:16px;text-align:right;">
        <option value="">${t().loading}</option>
      </select>
      <label class="chat-slot-label">${t().selectDay}</label>
      <div class="chat-slot-options" id="chat-slot-days">
        <button class="chat-slot-opt" data-day="today">${t().days.today}</button>
        <button class="chat-slot-opt" data-day="tomorrow">${t().days.tomorrow}</button>
        <button class="chat-slot-opt" data-day="after">${t().days.after}</button>
      </div>
      <label class="chat-slot-label">${t().selectTime}</label>
      <div class="chat-slot-options" id="chat-slot-times">
        <button class="chat-slot-opt" data-slot="morning">${t().slots.morning}</button>
        <button class="chat-slot-opt" data-slot="noon">${t().slots.noon}</button>
        <button class="chat-slot-opt" data-slot="afternoon">${t().slots.afternoon}</button>
        <button class="chat-slot-opt" data-slot="evening">${t().slots.evening}</button>
      </div>
      <button class="chat-slot-opt" style="width:100%;margin-top:16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ff27c9,#8d24ff);border:none;color:white;font-weight:900;" onclick="ChizenChat.confirmSlot()">${t().confirm}</button>
      <button class="chat-slot-close" style="margin-top:8px;" onclick="ChizenChat.closeSchedule()">✕</button>`;
    document.getElementById('chat-slot-modal').classList.add('active');
    loadUserOrders();
    state._slotSelected = null;
    state._daySelected = null;
    document.querySelectorAll('#chat-slot-days .chat-slot-opt').forEach(b => {
      b.onclick = () => { document.querySelectorAll('#chat-slot-days .chat-slot-opt').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('selected')); b.classList.add('selected'); state._daySelected = b.dataset.day; };
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#chat-slot-times .chat-slot-opt').forEach(b => {
      b.onclick = () => { document.querySelectorAll('#chat-slot-times .chat-slot-opt').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('selected')); b.classList.add('selected'); state._slotSelected = b.dataset.slot; };
    });
  }

  async function loadUserOrders() {
    const select = document.getElementById('chat-slot-order');
    try {
      if (!window.firebase || !firebase.auth) { select.innerHTML = '<option value="">غير متاح</option>'; return; }
      const uid = firebase.auth().currentUser?.uid;
      if (!uid) { select.innerHTML = `<option value="">${t().loginFirst}</option>`; return; }
      const snap = await firebase.firestore().collection('orders').where('userId', '==', uid).orderBy('createdAt', 'desc').limit(5).get();
      if (snap.empty) { select.innerHTML = `<option value="">${t().errorNoOrder}</option>`; return; }
      select.innerHTML = snap.docs.map(d => {
        const o = d.data();
        return `<option value="${d.id}">#${d.id.slice(0, 8)} — ${o.total} ريال</option>`;
      }).join('');
    } catch (e) {
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">فشل التحميل</option>';
    }
  }

  async function confirmSlot() {
    const orderId = document.getElementById('chat-slot-order').value;
    const slot = state._slotSelected;
    const day = state._daySelected;
    if (!orderId || !slot || !day) { alert(t().errorGeneric); return; }

    const date = new Date();
    if (day === 'tomorrow') date.setDate(date.getDate() + 1);
    if (day === 'after') date.setDate(date.getDate() + 2);
    const dateStr = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    try {
      await firebase.firestore().collection('orders').doc(orderId).update({
        deliverySlot: { slot, date: dateStr, scheduledAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }
      });
      closeSlotModal();
      addBubble('bot', t().slotBooked);
      try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_scheduled_delivery', JSON.stringify({ orderId, slot, date: dateStr })); } catch(e) {}
    } catch (e) {
      alert(t().slotFail);
    }
  }

  function closeSlotModal() { document.getElementById('chat-slot-modal').classList.remove('active'); }

  /* ═══ كوبون ترحيب — توليد محلي + حفظ في حساب المستخدم ═══ */
  async function claimWelcomeCoupon() {
    // 1. تحقق من الكوبون المحفوظ
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('chizen_welcome_coupon');
      if (saved) { showCouponBubble(saved); return; }
    } catch(e) {}

    // 2. تحقق من حساب المستخدم في Firestore
    try {
      const uid = firebase.auth()?.currentUser?.uid;
      if (uid) {
        const userDoc = await firebase.firestore().collection('users').doc(uid).get();
        if (userDoc.exists && userDoc.data().welcomeCoupon) {
          const code = userDoc.data().welcomeCoupon;
          try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_welcome_coupon', code); } catch(e) {}
          showCouponBubble(code);
          return;
        }
      }
    } catch(e) {}

    // 3. توليد كوبون جديد محلياً
    showTyping();
    setTimeout(async () => {
      hideTyping();
      const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
      let suffix = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) suffix += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
      const code = 'WELCOME-' + suffix;

      try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_welcome_coupon', code); } catch(e) {}

      try {
        const uid = firebase.auth()?.currentUser?.uid;
        if (uid) {
          await firebase.firestore().collection('users').doc(uid).set({
            welcomeCoupon: code,
            welcomeCouponIssued: true,
            welcomeCouponIssuedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
          }, { merge: true });
        }
      } catch(e) { console.warn('Save coupon:', e); }

      showCouponBubble(code);
    }, 600);
  }

  function showCouponBubble(code) {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'chat-msg bot';
    div.innerHTML = `
      <div class="chat-coupon-card">
        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:900;color:#e8eeff;">🎁 ${t().couponIssued}</div>
        <div class="chat-coupon-code">${esc(code)}</div>
        <div class="chat-coupon-hint">${t().couponHint}</div>
        <button style="margin-top:10px;padding:8px 16px;background:rgba(255,179,71,.2);border:1px solid rgba(255,179,71,.5);border-radius:8px;color:#ffb347;font-family:Tajawal,sans-serif;font-weight:900;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;" onclick="ChizenChat.copyCode('${esc(code)}')">📋 ${t().copied}</button>
      </div>`;
    state.chatMessages.appendChild(div);
    state.chatMessages.scrollTop = state.chatMessages.scrollHeight;
  }

  function copyCode(text) {
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => showToast(t().copied));
    else {
      const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value = text; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
      document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove(); showToast(t().copied);
    }
  }

  function showToast(msg) {
    let el = document.getElementById('chat-toast');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'chat-toast'; el.className = 'chat-toast'; document.body.appendChild(el); }
    el.textContent = msg;
    el.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(window._chatToastTimer);
    window._chatToastTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('show'), 2500);
  }

  function openWhatsApp() {
    let phone = WHATSAPP_NUMBER;
    try { const c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_contact') || '{}'); if (c.whatsapp) phone = c.whatsapp.replace(/\D/g, ''); } catch(e) {}
    window.open('https://wa.me/' + phone, '_blank');
  }

  window.ChizenChat = {
    open: openChat, close: closeChat,
    startAI: startAI, openWhatsApp: openWhatsApp,
    askQuick: function(text) { state.chatInput.value = text; sendMessage(); },
    claimCoupon: claimWelcomeCoupon,
    openSchedule: openSchedule, closeSchedule: closeSlotModal,
    confirmSlot: confirmSlot, toggleLang: toggleLang,
    sendMessage: sendMessage, copyCode: copyCode
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', createWidget);
  else createWidget();
})();

            '<div style="font-size:48px;">🎁</div>' +

              '<div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:900;color:var(--cr);">تم استلام طلبك!</div>' +

    if (!document.getElementById('sc-addresses')) {

            '<div style="padding:8px 20px 20px;" id="addr-list"></div>' +
            '<div style="padding:0 20px 20px;"><button class="btn-p" onclick="APP.addAddress()">+ إضافة عنوان جديد</button></div>' +

        );

    /* ═══ 8. شاشة طرق الدفع ═══ */
    if (!document.getElementById('sc-payment')) {
      var screens3 = document.getElementById('screens');
      if (screens3) {
        screens3.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
          '<div class="screen" id="sc-payment">' +
            '<div class="st"></div>' +
            '<div class="sh2"><button class="back" onclick="APP.goTab(\'profile\')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m9 18 6-6-6-6"/></svg></button><div class="st2">طرق الدفع</div></div>' +

          firstSit.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin',
            '<div class="sit" onclick="APP.goTab(\'addresses\')"><div class="sii"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"/><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"/></svg></div><div class="sit-t"><div class="sit-ti">عناويني</div></div><div class="mi-a">›</div></div>' +
            '<div class="sit" onclick="APP.goTab(\'payment\')"><div class="sii"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="1" y="4" width="22" height="16" rx="2"/><line x1="1" y1="10" x2="23" y2="10"/></svg></div><div class="sit-t"><div class="sit-ti">طرق الدفع</div></div><div class="mi-a">›</div></div>'
          );

    /* ═══ 10. تحسين شاشة التفاصيل (مفضلة + مشاركة + مميزات) ═══ */
    var detailsScreen = document.getElementById('sc-details');
    if (detailsScreen) {
      var detImg = document.getElementById('det-img');
      if (detImg && !detImg.querySelector('.fav-btn')) {
        detImg.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
          '<button id="fav-btn-det" style="position:absolute;top:16px;left:16px;width:42px;height:42px;background:rgba(2,5,27,.75);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;cursor:pointer;z-index:3;" onclick="APP.togFavDet()">' +
            '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:20px;height:20px;stroke:var(--pl);stroke-width:2;fill:none;"><path d="M19 14c1.49-1.46 3-3.21 3-5.5A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 16.5 3c-1.76 0-3 .5-4.5 2-1.5-1.5-2.74-2-4.5-2A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 2 8.5c0 2.3 1.5 4.05 3 5.5l7 7Z"/></svg>' +
          '</button>' +
          '<button style="position:absolute;top:16px;left:70px;width:42px;height:42px;background:rgba(2,5,27,.75);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;cursor:pointer;z-index:3;" onclick="APP.shareDet()">' +
            '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:18px;height:18px;stroke:var(--pl);stroke-width:2;fill:none;"><circle cx="18" cy="5" r="3"/><circle cx="6" cy="12" r="3"/><circle cx="18" cy="19" r="3"/><line x1="8.59" y1="13.51" x2="15.42" y2="17.49"/><line x1="15.41" y1="6.51" x2="8.59" y2="10.49"/></svg>' +

            '<div class="det-f" id="det-features" style="margin-top:16px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;"></div>'
          );
        }

    /* ═══ 11. إضافة زر المفضلة التفاصيل ═══ */
    A.togFavDet = function() {
      if (!this.currentProd) return;
      this.togFav(this.currentProd.id, document.getElementById('fav-btn-det'));
      var isFav = this.favorites.indexOf(this.currentProd.id) > -1;
      var btn = document.getElementById('fav-btn-det');
      if (btn) {
        var svg = btn.querySelector('svg');
        if (svg) {
          if (isFav) { svg.style.fill = 'var(--pk)'; svg.style.stroke = 'var(--pk)'; }
          else { svg.style.fill = 'none'; svg.style.stroke = 'var(--pl)'; }
        }
      }
    };

    /* ═══ 12. مشاركة المنتج ═══ */
    A.shareDet = function() {
      if (!this.currentProd) return;
      var t = 'شاهد ' + this.currentProd.name + ' على CHIZEN بسعر ' + this.currentProd.price + ' ر.س';
      if (navigator.share) navigator.share({title:'CHIZEN', text:t}).catch(function(){});
      else this.toast('المشاركة غير مدعومة');
    };

    /* ═══ 13. تحسين openProduct لعرض المميزات ═══ */
    var origOpen = A.openProduct;
    A.openProduct = function(id) {
      origOpen.call(this, id);
      var p = this.currentProd;
      if (p && p.features && p.features.length) {
        var fh = '';
        p.features.forEach(function(f) {
          fh += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:13px;color:var(--cr);"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:16px;height:16px;stroke:var(--gn);stroke-width:2.5;fill:none;flex-shrink:0;"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg>' + esc(f) + '</div>';
        });
        var fEl = document.getElementById('det-features');
        if (fEl) fEl.innerHTML = fh;
      }
      // تفعيل زر المفضلة
      var btn = document.getElementById('fav-btn-det');
      if (btn && p) {
        var svg = btn.querySelector('svg');
        var isFav = this.favorites.indexOf(p.id) > -1;
        if (svg) {
          if (isFav) { svg.style.fill = 'var(--pk)'; svg.style.stroke = 'var(--pk)'; }
          else { svg.style.fill = 'none'; svg.style.stroke = 'var(--pl)'; }
        }
      }
    };

    /* ═══ 14. عرض tracking widget ═══ */
    var origOnUserReady = A.onUserReady;
    A.onUserReady = function(u) {
      origOnUserReady.call(this, u);
      var self = this;
      setTimeout(function() {
        try { self.renderTrack && self.renderTrack(); } catch(e) {}
      }, 1200);
    };

    A.renderTrack = function() {
      var el = document.getElementById('tracking-w');
      if (!el) return;
      var po = null;
      for (var i = this.orders.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (this.orders[i].status === 'pending' || this.orders[i].status === 'shipped') { po = this.orders[i]; break; }
      }
      if (!po) { el.innerHTML = ''; return; }
      el.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:14px 16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(0,212,160,.13),rgba(0,223,255,.08));border:1px solid rgba(0,212,160,.3);border-radius:14px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.goTab(\'orders\')"><div style="width:42px;height:42px;background:rgba(0,212,160,.2);border-radius:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:20px;">🚚</div><div style="flex:1;"><div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:900;color:var(--cr);">طلبك #' + esc((po.id||'').substring(0,10)) + ' قيد التتبع</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mt);margin-top:2px;">اضغط لتتبع</div></div><div style="color:var(--gn);font-size:20px;font-weight:900;">›</div></div>';
    };

    /* ═══ 15. عرض العروض الخاطفة ═══ */
    A.renderFlash = function() {
      var c = document.getElementById('flash-c');
      var sec = document.getElementById('flash-sec');
      if (!c) return;
      var items = this.products.filter(function(p) { return p.old && (p.old - p.price) / p.old > 0.15; }).slice(0, 6);
      if (!items.length) { if (sec) sec.style.display = 'none'; return; }
      if (sec) sec.style.display = 'block';
      c.innerHTML = items.map(function(p) {
        return '<div style="flex-shrink:0;width:130px;background:var(--nav);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.openProduct(\'' + esc(p.id) + '\')"><div style="height:100px;position:relative;overflow:hidden;"><img src="' + esc(p.img||'') + '" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;"><div style="position:absolute;top:6px;right:6px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ff4d6d,#ff8b45);color:#fff;font-size:9px;font-weight:900;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:6px;">⚡ ' + esc(p.badge||'') + '</div></div><div style="padding:8px;"><div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--cr);margin-bottom:4px;line-height:1.3;height:28px;overflow:hidden;">' + esc(p.name) + '</div><div style="display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:4px;"><span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:900;color:var(--pl);">' + esc(p.price) + '</span><span style="font-size:9px;color:var(--mt2);text-decoration:line-through;">' + esc(p.old) + '</span></div></div></div>';
      }).join('');
    };

    /* ═══ 16. عرض العلامات التجارية ═══ */
    A.renderBrands = function() {
      var c = document.getElementById('brands');
      var sec = document.getElementById('brands-sec');
      if (!c) return;
      var brands = {};
      this.products.forEach(function(p) { if (p.brand) brands[p.brand] = true; });
      var brandList = Object.keys(brands);
      if (!brandList.length) { if (sec) sec.style.display = 'none'; return; }
      if (sec) sec.style.display = 'block';
      c.innerHTML = '<div style="flex-shrink:0;padding:10px 20px;background:var(--grad2);color:#fff;border-radius:14px;font-size:13px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.filterBrand(\'all\')">الكل</div>' +
        brandList.map(function(b) { return '<div style="flex-shrink:0;padding:10px 20px;background:var(--nav);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:var(--cr);cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.filterBrand(\'' + esc(b) + '\')">' + esc(b) + '</div>'; }).join('');
    };

    A.filterBrand = function(b) {
      if (b === 'all') { this.renderProducts(this.products); return; }
      this.renderProducts(this.products.filter(function(p) { return p.brand === b; }));
    };

    /* ═══ 17. طرق دفع في صفحة payment ═══ */
    A.renderPaymentMethods = function() {
      var c = document.getElementById('pay-list');
      if (!c) return;
      c.innerHTML = this.banks.map(function(b) {
        return '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:16px;background:var(--nav);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:10px;"><div style="width:44px;height:44px;background:' + esc(b.color||'#333') + '20;border-radius:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:20px;">' + esc(b.icon||'🏦') + '</div><div style="flex:1;"><div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--cr);">' + esc(b.name) + '</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mt);margin-top:2px;">' + esc(b.account||'ادفع عند الاستلام') + '</div></div></div>';
      }).join('');
    };

    /* ═══ 18. العناوين ═══ */
    A.renderAddresses = function() {
      var c = document.getElementById('addr-list');
      if (!c) return;
      var list = [];
      try { list = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_addresses') || '[]'); } catch(e) {}
      if (!list.length) { c.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--mt);">لا توجد عناوين</div>'; return; }
      c.innerHTML = list.map(function(a, i) {
        return '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:16px;background:var(--nav);border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:10px;"><div style="flex:1;"><div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--cr);">' + esc(a.name||'') + '</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mt);margin-top:2px;">' + esc(a.detail||'') + '</div></div><button style="background:rgba(255,77,109,.15);border:1px solid rgba(255,77,109,.4);color:var(--rd);border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer;" onclick="APP.deleteAddress(' + i + ')">🗑️</button></div>';
      }).join('');
    };

    A.addAddress = function() {
      var self = this;
      this.showPrompt('اسم العنوان', '', function(name) {
        if (!name) return;
        self.showPrompt('العنوان الكامل', '', function(detail) {
          if (!detail) return;
          var list = [];
          try { list = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_addresses') || '[]'); } catch(e) {}
          list.push({name: name, detail: detail});
          try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_addresses', JSON.stringify(list)); } catch(e) {}
          self.renderAddresses();
          self.toast('✓ تمت الإضافة');
        });
      });
    };

    A.deleteAddress = function(i) {
      var self = this;
      var list = [];
      try { list = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_addresses') || '[]'); } catch(e) {}
      list.splice(i, 1);
      try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_addresses', JSON.stringify(list)); } catch(e) {}
      self.renderAddresses();
      self.toast('تم الحذف');
    };

    /* ═══ 19. ربط الشاشات الجديدة بـ navigate ═══ */
    var origNavigate = A.navigate;
    A.navigate = function(id, push) {
      origNavigate.call(this, id, push);
      if (id === 'addresses') this.renderAddresses();
      if (id === 'payment') this.renderPaymentMethods();
    };

    /* ═══ 20. إعادة عرض كل شيء بعد 500ms ═══ */
    setTimeout(function() {
      try { A.renderCats(); } catch(e) {}
      try { A.renderBanners(); } catch(e) {}
      try { A.renderProducts(); } catch(e) {}
      try { A.renderFlash && A.renderFlash(); } catch(e) {}
      try { A.renderBrands && A.renderBrands(); } catch(e) {}
      try { A.updateCartB && A.updateCartB(); } catch(e) {}
      try { A.updateNotifB && A.updateNotifB(); } catch(e) {}
    }, 600);

    /* ═══ 21. شاشة النجاح بعد الطلب ═══ */
    var origConfirm = A.confirmOrder;
    A.confirmOrder = async function() {
      var before = this.orders.length;
      await origConfirm.call(this);
      if (this.orders.length > before) {
        var last = this.orders[this.orders.length - 1];
        var succ = document.getElementById('succ-oid');
        if (succ) succ.textContent = '#' + last.id;
        this.navigate('success', true);
      }
    };

    console.log('✅ CHIZEN Extra Applied (16 fixes)');
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { setTimeout(patch, 300); });
  } else {
    setTimeout(patch, 300);
  }
})();
