/* CHIZEN — Firebase Service v4 (Ready immediately) */
window.esc = function esc(s) {
  return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c){
    return { '&':'&amp;', '<':'&lt;', '>':'&gt;', '"':'&quot;', "'":'&#39;' }[c];
  });
};

window.FB = {
  app: null, auth: null, db: null, ready: false, currentUser: null,

  init: async function() {
    // ═══ فحص سريع للتبعيات ═══
    if (!window.firebase) {
      console.error('[FB] Firebase SDK غير محمّل');
      this.ready = true;
      return false;
    }
    if (!window.FIREBASE_CONFIG || !window.FIREBASE_CONFIG.apiKey) {
      console.error('[FB] Firebase config مفقود');
      this.ready = true;
      return false;
    }

    try {
      this.app = (!firebase.apps.length) ? firebase.initializeApp(window.FIREBASE_CONFIG) : firebase.app();
      this.auth = firebase.auth();
      this.db = firebase.firestore();

      // ═══ الأهم: ready = true فوراً قبل أي await ═══
      this.ready = true;
      console.log('[FB] Ready immediately');

      // ═══ كل ما يلي يعمل في الخلفية بدون تعطيل الواجهة ═══
      try {
        this.auth.setPersistence(firebase.auth.Auth.Persistence.LOCAL).then(function(){
          console.log('[FB] Persistence LOCAL ✓');
        }).catch(function(e){ console.warn('[FB] Persistence:', e); });
      } catch(e) {}

      try {
        this.db.enablePersistence().catch(function(e){ console.warn('[FB] db:', e.code); });
      } catch(e) {}

      var self = this;
      try {
        this.auth.onAuthStateChanged(function(user) {
          self.currentUser = user;
          console.log('[FB] User:', user ? user.email : 'null');
        });
      } catch(e) { console.warn('[FB] onAuth:', e); }

      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[FB] Init failed:', e);
      this.ready = true;
      return false;
    }
  },

  onAuthChange: function(cb) {
    if (!this.auth) { cb(null); return; }
    this.auth.onAuthStateChanged(function(user){ FB.currentUser = user; cb(user); });
  },
  loginEmail: async function(email, password) {
    if (!this.auth) throw { code: 'auth/not-ready', message: 'Firebase غير جاهز' };
    return await this.auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email, password);
  },
  signupEmail: async function(email, password) {
    if (!this.auth) throw { code: 'auth/not-ready', message: 'Firebase غير جاهز' };
    return await this.auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email, password);
  },
  loginGoogle: async function() {
    if (!this.auth) throw { code: 'auth/not-ready', message: 'Firebase غير جاهز' };
    var provider = new firebase.auth.GoogleAuthProvider();
    return await this.auth.signInWithPopup(provider);
  },
  handleGoogleRedirect: async function() {
    if (!this.auth) return null;
    try { var r = await this.auth.getRedirectResult(); return (r && r.user) ? r.user : null; } catch(e) { return null; }
  },
  logout: async function() { if (!this.auth) return; await this.auth.signOut(); },
  updateDisplayName: async function(name) {
    if (!this.currentUser) return;
    await this.currentUser.updateProfile({ displayName: name });
    this.currentUser = this.auth.currentUser;
  },
  sendPasswordReset: async function(email) {
    if (!this.auth) throw { code: 'auth/not-ready', message: 'Firebase غير جاهز' };
    return await this.auth.sendPasswordResetEmail(email);
  },
  getUserId: function() { return this.currentUser ? this.currentUser.uid : null; },
  getUserEmail: function() { return this.currentUser ? this.currentUser.email : null; },
  getUserName: function() { return this.currentUser ? (this.currentUser.displayName || '') : ''; },

  saveUserProfile: async function(data) {
    if (!this.db || !this.currentUser) return;
    data.updatedAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    data.email = this.currentUser.email;
    data.uid = this.currentUser.uid;
    await this.db.collection('users').doc(this.currentUser.uid).set(data, { merge: true });
  },
  getUserProfile: async function() {
    if (!this.db || !this.currentUser) return null;
    try { var doc = await this.db.collection('users').doc(this.currentUser.uid).get(); return doc.exists ? doc.data() : null; } catch(e) { return null; }
  },
  getAllUsers: async function() {
    if (!this.db) return [];
    try { var snap = await this.db.collection('users').limit(500).get(); var arr = []; snap.forEach(function(doc){ var d = doc.data(); d.uid = doc.id; arr.push(d); }); return arr; } catch(e) { return []; }
  },
  deleteUser: async function(uid) { if (!this.db) return; await this.db.collection('users').doc(uid).delete(); },

  getProducts: async function() {
    if (!this.db) return [];
    try { var snap = await this.db.collection('products').limit(500).get(); var arr = []; snap.forEach(function(doc){ var d = doc.data(); d.id = doc.id; arr.push(d); }); return arr; } catch(e) { return []; }
  },
  addProduct: async function(p) {
    if (!this.db) throw new Error('Firebase not ready');
    var pl = Object.assign({}, p); delete pl.id;
    pl.createdAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    var ref = await this.db.collection('products').add(pl); return ref.id;
  },
  updateProduct: async function(id, p) {
    if (!this.db) throw new Error('Firebase not ready');
    var pl = Object.assign({}, p); delete pl.id;
    pl.updatedAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    await this.db.collection('products').doc(id).update(pl);
  },
  deleteProduct: async function(id) { if (!this.db) return; await this.db.collection('products').doc(id).delete(); },

  getBanners: async function() {
    if (!this.db) return null;
    try { var doc = await this.db.collection('config').doc('banners').get(); if (!doc.exists) return null; var d = doc.data(); return (d && d.list && d.list.length) ? d.list : null; } catch(e) { return null; }
  },
  setBanners: async function(list) {
    if (!this.db) return;
    await this.db.collection('config').doc('banners').set({ list: list, updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true });
  },
  getConfig: async function(key) {
    if (!this.db) return null;
    try { var doc = await this.db.collection('config').doc(key).get(); return doc.exists ? doc.data() : null; } catch(e) { return null; }
  },
  setConfig: async function(key, data) {
    if (!this.db) return;
    var pl = Object.assign({}, data);
    pl.updatedAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    await this.db.collection('config').doc(key).set(pl, { merge: true });
  },
  getDeal: async function() {
    if (!this.db) return null;
    try { var doc = await this.db.collection('config').doc('deal').get(); return doc.exists ? doc.data() : null; } catch(e) { return null; }
  },
  setDeal: async function(deal) {
    if (!this.db) return;
    var pl = Object.assign({}, deal);
    pl.updatedAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    await this.db.collection('config').doc('deal').set(pl, { merge: true });
  },
  deleteDeal: async function() { if (!this.db) return; await this.db.collection('config').doc('deal').delete(); },

  getUserOrders: async function() {
    if (!this.db || !this.currentUser) return [];
    try {
      var snap = await this.db.collection('orders').where('userId','==',this.currentUser.uid).limit(200).get();
      var arr = []; snap.forEach(function(doc){ var d = doc.data(); d.id = doc.id; arr.push(d); });
      arr.sort(function(a,b){ return ((b.createdAt&&b.createdAt.seconds)||0) - ((a.createdAt&&a.createdAt.seconds)||0); });
      return arr;
    } catch(e) { return []; }
  },
  getAllOrders: async function() {
    if (!this.db) return [];
    try { var snap = await this.db.collection('orders').limit(300).get(); var arr = []; snap.forEach(function(doc){ var d = doc.data(); d.id = doc.id; arr.push(d); }); return arr; } catch(e) { return []; }
  },
  addOrder: async function(order) {
    if (!this.db) throw new Error('Firebase not ready');
    order.userId = this.getUserId(); order.userEmail = this.getUserEmail();
    order.createdAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    var ref = await this.db.collection('orders').add(order); return ref.id;
  },
  updateOrderStatus: async function(id, status) {
    if (!this.db) return;
    await this.db.collection('orders').doc(id).update({ status: status, updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() });
  },
  deleteOrder: async function(id) { if (!this.db) return; await this.db.collection('orders').doc(id).delete(); },

  getNotifications: async function() {
    if (!this.db || !this.currentUser) return [];
    try {
      var a1 = await this.db.collection('notifications').where('userId','==',this.currentUser.uid).limit(50).get();
      var a2 = await this.db.collection('notifications').where('userId','==','ALL').limit(50).get();
      var arr = []; a1.forEach(function(doc){ var d = doc.data(); d.id = doc.id; arr.push(d); }); a2.forEach(function(doc){ var d = doc.data(); d.id = doc.id; arr.push(d); });
      arr.sort(function(a,b){ return ((b.createdAt&&b.createdAt.seconds)||0) - ((a.createdAt&&a.createdAt.seconds)||0); });
      return arr.slice(0, 60);
    } catch(e) { return []; }
  },
  addNotification: async function(notif) {
    if (!this.db) return;
    var pl = Object.assign({ userId: 'ALL' }, notif);
    pl.createdAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    await this.db.collection('notifications').add(pl);
  },
  addUserNotification: async function(uid, notif) {
    if (!this.db) return;
    var pl = Object.assign({ userId: uid }, notif);
    pl.createdAt = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    await this.db.collection('notifications').add(pl);
  },

  updateUserPoints: async function(points) {
    if (!this.db || !this.currentUser) return;
    await this.db.collection('users').doc(this.currentUser.uid).set({ loyaltyPoints: points, updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true });
  }
};

if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){ FB.init(); });
else FB.init();

===== FILE: notification-sound.js =====
/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════
   CHIZEN NOTIFICATION SOUND
   صوت مخصص يُولّد برمجياً — بدون ملف صوتي
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */

window.ChizenSound = {
  
  audioCtx: null,
  
  init: function() {
    if (!this.audioCtx) {
      try {
        var AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
        if (AC) this.audioCtx = new AC();
      } catch (e) { console.log('[Sound] AudioContext not supported'); }
    }
  },
  
  play: function() {
    this.init();
    if (!this.audioCtx) return;
    if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') this.audioCtx.resume();
    
    var ctx = this.audioCtx;
    var now = ctx.currentTime;
    
    this.playNote(523.25, now, 0.25, 0.15);
    this.playNote(659.25, now + 0.12, 0.25, 0.15);
    this.playNote(783.99, now + 0.24, 0.35, 0.20);
    this.playNote(1046.50, now + 0.30, 0.4, 0.05);
  },
  
  playNote: function(freq, startTime, duration, volume) {
    var ctx = this.audioCtx;
    
    var osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    
    var osc2 = ctx.createOscillator();
    osc2.type = 'triangle';
    osc2.frequency.value = freq * 2;
    
    var filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    filter.type = 'lowpass';
    filter.frequency.value = 2500;
    
    var gain = ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, startTime);
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(volume, startTime + 0.02);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, startTime + duration);
    
    osc.connect(filter);
    osc2.connect(filter);
    filter.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    
    osc.start(startTime);
    osc2.start(startTime);
    osc.stop(startTime + duration);
    osc2.stop(startTime + duration);
  },
  
  playOrder: function() {
    this.init();
    if (!this.audioCtx) return;
    if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') this.audioCtx.resume();
    
    var ctx = this.audioCtx;
    var now = ctx.currentTime;
    
    this.playNote(659.25, now, 0.2, 0.15);
    this.playNote(783.99, now + 0.15, 0.2, 0.15);
    this.playNote(1046.50, now + 0.30, 0.5, 0.20);
  },
  
  playOffer: function() {
    this.init();
    if (!this.audioCtx) return;
    if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') this.audioCtx.resume();
    
    var ctx = this.audioCtx;
    var now = ctx.currentTime;
    
    this.playNote(523.25, now, 0.15, 0.12);
    this.playNote(659.25, now + 0.08, 0.15, 0.14);
    this.playNote(783.99, now + 0.16, 0.15, 0.16);
    this.playNote(1046.50, now + 0.24, 0.4, 0.18);
  },
  
  playPoints: function() {
    this.init();
    if (!this.audioCtx) return;
    if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') this.audioCtx.resume();
    
    var ctx = this.audioCtx;
    var now = ctx.currentTime;
    
    this.playNote(880, now, 0.2, 0.12);
    this.playNote(1174.66, now + 0.12, 0.4, 0.15);
  }
};

document.addEventListener('touchstart', function once() {
  ChizenSound.init();
  document.removeEventListener('touchstart', once);
}, { once: true });

document.addEventListener('click', function once() {
  ChizenSound.init();
  document.removeEventListener('click', once);
}, { once: true });
===== FILE: notifications.js =====
/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════
   CHIZEN NOTIFICATIONS v3
   Push حقيقي + محلي + Capacitor Native
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */

window.ChizenNotif = {
  platform: 'web',
  fcmToken: null,
  messaging: null,

  init: function() {
    /* كشف المنصة */
    if (window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform && window.Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
      this.platform = 'native';
      this.initNative();
    } else if (window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.Plugins && window.Capacitor.Plugins.PushNotifications) {
      this.platform = 'native';
      this.initNative();
    } else if ('Notification' in window && 'serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      this.platform = 'web';
      this.initWebFCM();
    }
  },

  /* ═══ Native (Capacitor APK) — إشعارات حقيقية خارجية ═══ */
  initNative: async function() {
    try {
      var P = window.Capacitor.Plugins.PushNotifications;
      if (!P) { this.platform = 'web'; this.initWebFCM(); return; }

      /* 1. طلب الإذن */
      var perm = await P.requestPermissions();
      if (perm.receive !== 'granted') {
        console.log('[Notif] الإذن مرفوض');
        return;
      }

      /* 2. تسجيل مع FCM */
      await P.register();

      /* 3. استقبال التوكن */
      P.addListener('registration', function(token) {
        console.log('[Notif] FCM Token:', token.value);
        ChizenNotif.fcmToken = token.value;
        try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_fcm_token', token.value); } catch(e) {}

        /* حفظ في Firestore */
        if (window.FB && FB.ready && FB.currentUser) {
          FB.saveUserProfile({ fcmToken: token.value }).catch(function(){});
        }

        /* حفظ بعد تسجيل الدخول إن لم يكن مسجلاً بعد */
        if (!FB.currentUser) {
          setTimeout(function() {
            if (window.FB && FB.ready && FB.currentUser) {
              FB.saveUserProfile({ fcmToken: token.value }).catch(function(){});
            }
          }, 3000);
        }
      });

      P.addListener('registrationError', function(err) {
        console.error('[Notif] Registration error:', err);
      });

      /* 4. استقبال إشعار والتطبيق مفتوح (Foreground) */
      P.addListener('pushNotificationReceived', function(notification) {
        console.log('[Notif] Foreground:', notification);
        var title = notification.title || 'CHIZEN';
        var body  = notification.body || '';
        var data  = notification.data || {};
        ChizenNotif.send(title, body, { type: data.type || 'push' });
      });

      /* 5. عند نقر الإشعار (خلفية/مغلق) */
      P.addListener('pushNotificationActionPerformed', function(action) {
        console.log('[Notif] Action:', action);
        var data = action.notification.data || {};
        try {
          if (data.type === 'order') {
            window.location.href = 'chizen.html#orders';
          } else if (data.type === 'product' && data.productId) {
            localStorage.setItem('chizen_open_product', data.productId);
            window.location.href = 'chizen.html';
          } else {
            window.location.href = 'chizen.html';
          }
        } catch(e) {}
      });

      console.log('[Notif] Native initialized ✓');

    } catch(e) {
      console.error('[Notif] Native init failed:', e);
      this.platform = 'web';
      this.initWebFCM();
    }
  },

  /* ═══ Web + FCM ═══ */
  initWebFCM: async function() {
    try {
      if (!window.firebase || !window.firebase.messaging) {
        console.log('[Notif] FCM SDK غير محمّل — إشعارات محلية فقط');
        return;
      }
      if (Notification.permission !== 'granted') return;

      var reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
      this.messaging = firebase.messaging();

      try { await this.messaging.useServiceWorker(reg); } catch(e) {}

      var vapidKey = window.FCM_VAPID_KEY || '';
      if (!vapidKey) { console.log('[Notif] VAPID key ناقص'); return; }

      this.fcmToken = await this.messaging.getToken({ vapidKey: vapidKey });
      if (this.fcmToken) {
        try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_fcm_token', this.fcmToken); } catch(e) {}
        if (window.FB && FB.ready && FB.currentUser) {
          FB.saveUserProfile({ fcmToken: this.fcmToken }).catch(function(){});
        }
      }

      this.messaging.onMessage(function(payload) {
        var n = payload.notification || {};
        var d = payload.data || {};
        ChizenNotif.send(n.title || d.title || 'CHIZEN', n.body || d.body || '', {
          type: d.type || 'push', productId: d.productId, orderId: d.orderId
        });
      });

      this.messaging.onTokenRefresh(async function() {
        try {
          var newToken = await ChizenNotif.messaging.getToken();
          ChizenNotif.fcmToken = newToken;
          try { localStorage.setItem('chizen_fcm_token', newToken); } catch(e) {}
          if (window.FB && FB.ready && FB.currentUser) {
            FB.saveUserProfile({ fcmToken: newToken }).catch(function(){});
          }
        } catch(e){}
      });

    } catch(e) {
      console.log('[Notif] FCM init failed:', e.message);
    }
  },

  /* ═══ طلب الإذن ═══ */
  requestPermission: async function() {
    if (this.platform === 'native') {
      try {
        var P = window.Capacitor.Plugins.PushNotifications;
        var p = await P.requestPermissions();
        return p.receive === 'granted';
      } catch(e) { return false; }
    } else if ('Notification' in window) {
      if (Notification.permission === 'granted') { this.initWebFCM(); return true; }
      if (Notification.permission === 'denied') return false;
      var r = await Notification.requestPermission();
      if (r === 'granted') { this.initWebFCM(); return true; }
      return false;
    }
    return false;
  },

  /* ═══ إرسال إشعار محلي ═══ */
  send: function(title, body, options) {
    options = options || {};

    /* احترم إعدادات المستخدم */
    try {
      var st = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_state') || '{}');
      if (st.notifications === false) return;
      var t0 = options.type || 'general';
      if (t0 === 'offer' && st.offers === false) return;
      if (t0 === 'order' && st.orderNotifs === false) return;
    } catch(e) {}

    this.saveLocal(title, body, options);

    /* صوت */
    if (window.ChizenSound) {
      var t = options.type || 'general';
      if (t === 'order') ChizenSound.playOrder();
      else if (t === 'offer' || t === 'deal') ChizenSound.playOffer();
      else if (t === 'points') ChizenSound.playPoints();
      else ChizenSound.play();
    }

    /* إشعار نظام للويب فقط */
    if (this.platform === 'web' && 'Notification' in window && Notification.permission === 'granted') {
      try {
        var notifOptions = {
          body: body,
          tag: options.tag || 'chizen-' + Date.now(),
          requireInteraction: false,
          silent: true,
          vibrate: options.vibrate !== false ? [200, 100, 200] : undefined
        };
        if (navigator.serviceWorker && navigator.serviceWorker.controller) {
          navigator.serviceWorker.ready.then(function(reg) {
            reg.showNotification(title, notifOptions);
          });
        } else {
          new Notification(title, notifOptions);
        }
      } catch(e) {}
    }

    /* Toast داخل التطبيق */
    if (window.APP && APP.toast) APP.toast(body);
  },

  saveLocal: function(title, body, options) {
    try {
      var list = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('chizen_notifications') || '[]');
      list.push({
        title: title, body: body,
        date: new Date().toISOString(),
        type: options.type || 'general',
        read: false
      });
      if (list.length > 100) list.shift();
      localStorage.setItem('chizen_notifications', JSON.stringify(list));
      if (window.APP) {
        APP.notifications = list;
        if (APP.updateNotifBadge) APP.updateNotifBadge();
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  },

  /* ═══ إشعارات جاهزة ═══ */
  orderPlaced: function(orderId, total) {
    this.send('تم استلام طلبك ✓', 'طلب #' + orderId + ' بقيمة ' + total + ' ر.س',
      { type: 'order', tag: 'order-' + orderId });
  },
  orderConfirmed: function(orderId) {
    this.send('تم تأكيد طلبك', 'طلب #' + orderId + ' قيد التجهيز الآن',
      { type: 'order', tag: 'confirm-' + orderId });
  },
  orderShipped: function(orderId) {
    this.send('طلبك في الطريق 🚚', 'طلب #' + orderId + ' خرج من المستودع',
      { type: 'order', tag: 'ship-' + orderId, vibrate: [300,100,300,100,300] });
  },
  orderDelivered: function(orderId) {
    this.send('وصل طلبك 🎉', 'طلب #' + orderId + ' جاهز للاستلام',
      { type: 'order', tag: 'deliver-' + orderId });
  },
  offer: function(title, body, productId) {
    this.send('🔥 ' + title, body,
      { type: 'offer', tag: 'offer-' + Date.now(), productId: productId });
  },
  pointsEarned: function(points) {
    this.send('💎 نقاط جديدة!', 'حصلت على ' + points + ' نقطة',
      { type: 'points', tag: 'points-' + Date.now() });
  },
  welcome: function(name) {
    this.send('أهلاً ' + name + ' 👋', 'مرحباً بك في CHIZEN',
      { type: 'welcome', tag: 'welcome-' + Date.now() });
  }
};

if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){ ChizenNotif.init(); });
} else {
  ChizenNotif.init();
}
===== FILE: sw.js =====
// سكربت لإجبار التطبيق على مسح الكاش القديم وتحديث نفسه
self.addEventListener('install', function(event) {
  self.skipWaiting(); // تفعيل فوري
});

self.addEventListener('activate', function(event) {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then(function(cacheNames) {
      return Promise.all(
        cacheNames.map(function(cacheName) {
          return caches.delete(cacheName); // مسح كل الكاش القديم
        })
      );
    }).then(function() {
      return self.clients.claim();
    })
  );
});

// استراتيجية: جلب من الإنترنت دائماً، وإذا انقطع، استخدم الكاش
self.addEventListener('fetch', function(event) {
  if (event.request.method !== 'GET') return;
  event.respondWith(
    fetch(event.request).catch(function() {
      return caches.match(event.request);
    })
  );
});
